#+PROPERTY: header-args:emacs-lisp :tangle ./init.el

* DONE Core Emacs
Foundational layer of the config: bootstrap sequence, package management, performance tuning, session persistence, and cross-platform env normalization. Tuned for Emacs 30/31 — responsive, OS-safe, and stutter-free under heavy IDE workloads (LSP, Tree-sitter).

** DONE Lexical Binding
Lexical binding is a strict requirement for modern Emacs Lisp. Unlike dynamic binding, it scopes variables to their enclosing block, enabling closures, preventing variable-bleeding bugs, and letting the byte-compiler generate faster, optimized code.

The =no-byte-compile: t= header directive stops Emacs from prematurely compiling this tangled file before =compile-angel= registers its exclusion rules.

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;;; init.el --- Main Configuration File -*- no-byte-compile: t; lexical-binding: t; -*-
;;; Commentary:
;; This file is auto-generated by tangling `config.org`.
;; Lexical binding is enabled globally for this file via the header above.
#+end_src


** DONE Package Management
Initializes =elpaca=, an asynchronous package manager replacing =package.el=. Directory structure adheres to the =no-littering= paradigm, redirecting repositories and builds to =var/elpaca/=.

*Load-Order Physics & Asynchronous Safety:*
- =elpaca-use-package=: Installed and blocked via =(elpaca-wait)=. Ensures =:ensure= advice applies to native =use-package= prior to subsequent macro-expansion.
- =no-littering=: Installed with =:ensure (:wait t)=. Enforces XDG-like paths before asynchronously queued packages evaluate cache directories.
- =custom.el=: Deferred to =elpaca-after-init-hook=. Guarantees execution post-queue processing.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; Prefer newer source files over stale byte-compiled artifacts.
(setq load-prefer-newer t)

;; Ensure use-package macro availability during byte-compilation.
(eval-when-compile (require 'use-package))
(require 'bind-key)

;; ELPACA BOOTSTRAP
(defvar elpaca-installer-version 0.12)
;; Redirect Elpaca root to var/elpaca/ (no-littering paradigm).
(defvar elpaca-directory (expand-file-name "var/elpaca/" user-emacs-directory))
(defvar elpaca-builds-directory (expand-file-name "builds/" elpaca-directory))
(defvar elpaca-sources-directory (expand-file-name "sources/" elpaca-directory))
(defvar elpaca-order '(elpaca :repo "https://github.com/progfolio/elpaca.git"
                              :ref nil :depth 1 :inherit ignore
                              :files (:defaults "elpaca-test.el" (:exclude "extensions"))
                              :build (:not elpaca-activate)))
(let* ((repo  (expand-file-name "elpaca/" elpaca-sources-directory))
       (build (expand-file-name "elpaca/" elpaca-builds-directory))
       (order (cdr elpaca-order))
       (default-directory repo))
  (add-to-list 'load-path (if (file-exists-p build) build repo))
  (unless (file-exists-p repo)
    (make-directory repo t)
    (when (<= emacs-major-version 28) (require 'subr-x))
    (condition-case-unless-debug err
        (if-let* ((buffer (pop-to-buffer-same-window "*elpaca-bootstrap*"))
                  ((zerop (apply #'call-process `("git" nil ,buffer t "clone"
                                                  ,@(when-let* ((depth (plist-get order :depth)))
                                                      (list (format "--depth=%d" depth) "--no-single-branch"))
                                                  ,(plist-get order :repo) ,repo))))
                  ((zerop (call-process "git" nil buffer t "checkout"
                                        (or (plist-get order :ref) "--"))))
                  (emacs (concat invocation-directory invocation-name))
                  ((zerop (call-process emacs nil buffer nil "-Q" "-L" "." "--batch"
                                        "--eval" "(byte-recompile-directory \".\" 0 'force)")))
                  ((require 'elpaca))
                  ((elpaca-generate-autoloads "elpaca" repo)))
            (progn (message "%s" (buffer-string)) (kill-buffer buffer))
          (error "%s" (with-current-buffer buffer (buffer-string))))
      ((error) (warn "%s" err) (delete-directory repo 'recursive))))
  (unless (require 'elpaca-autoloads nil t)
    (require 'elpaca)
    (elpaca-generate-autoloads "elpaca" repo)
    (let ((load-source-file-function nil)) (load "./elpaca-autoloads"))))
;; Exception: Internal bootstrap hook must remain `after-init-hook`.
(add-hook 'after-init-hook #'elpaca-process-queues)
(elpaca `(,@elpaca-order))

;; Use Package Integration
(elpaca elpaca-use-package
  (elpaca-use-package-mode)
  (setq elpaca-use-package-by-default t))

;; Block init reading until elpaca-use-package advice is applied.
(elpaca-wait)

;; Use Package Configuration
(setq use-package-verbose nil
      use-package-always-ensure t
      use-package-expand-minimally t
      use-package-compute-statistics nil
      use-package-enable-imenu-support t)

;; NO-LITTERING & CUSTOM FILE
;; Synchronously load no-littering to preempt async package cache paths.
(use-package no-littering
  :ensure (:wait t)
  :demand t
  :config
  (setq custom-file (no-littering-expand-etc-file-name "custom.el"))
  ;; Defer custom.el loading until Elpaca queues finish processing.
  (add-hook 'elpaca-after-init-hook (lambda () (load custom-file 'noerror)))
  (no-littering-theme-backups))

;; Register local personal libraries.
(add-to-list 'load-path (expand-file-name "lisp" user-emacs-directory))
#+end_src



** DONE Custom Functions
Utility functions for environment validation, performance guards, and configuration reloading. Includes childframe and icon rendering checks for UI dependencies, a buffer-size threshold to suspend expensive minor modes, an Elpaca-aware configuration reloader, and a region/symbol fallback for text operations.

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defun childframe-workable-p ()
  "Return non-nil if childframes are supported.
Requires a graphical display or terminal with childframe capabilities.
Utilized by UI packages such as `corfu' and `posframe'."
  (and (not noninteractive)
       (or (display-graphic-p)
           (featurep 'tty-child-frames))
       (eq (frame-parameter (selected-frame) 'minibuffer) 't)))

(defun childframe-completion-workable-p ()
  "Return non-nil if childframe completion is supported."
  (childframe-workable-p))

(defun icons-displayable-p ()
  "Return non-nil if icon fonts are displayable.
Prevents rendering attempts in environments lacking `nerd-icons' support."
  (or (featurep 'nerd-icons)
      (require 'nerd-icons nil t)))

(defun too-long-file-p ()
  "Return non-nil if the current buffer exceeds size thresholds.
Disables expensive minor modes in massive buffers to prevent main-thread freezing."
  (or (> (buffer-size) 500000)
      (and (fboundp 'buffer-line-statistics)
           (> (car (buffer-line-statistics)) 10000))))

(defun reload-init-file ()
  "Tangle and reload the Emacs configuration from `config.org'.
Processes Elpaca's asynchronous queues post-reload. Emacs cannot natively
unload packages or clear modified hooks; a full restart is required for
structural teardown."
  (interactive)
  (let* ((config-org (expand-file-name "config.org" user-emacs-directory))
         (init-el (expand-file-name "init.el" user-emacs-directory))
         (start-time (current-time))
         (divider (propertize "━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━" 'face 'shadow)))

    (unless (file-exists-p config-org)
      (user-error "Configuration file %s not found" config-org))

    (when (y-or-n-p "Reload configuration? (Teardown requires restart) ")
      (message "%s" divider)
      (message "%s" (propertize "Tangling config.org..." 'face 'bold))
      (require 'org)

      (condition-case err
          (org-babel-tangle-file config-org init-el "emacs-lisp")
        (error
         (user-error "Tangle failed: %s" (error-message-string err))))

      (message "%s %s" (propertize "✓" 'face 'success) "Tangled")
      (message "%s" (propertize "Loading init.el..." 'face 'bold))

      (condition-case err
          (load-file init-el)
        (error
         (user-error "Load failed: %s" (error-message-string err))))

      ;; Process Elpaca queues to resolve newly added dependencies.
      (when (fboundp 'elpaca-process-queues)
        (message "%s" (propertize "Processing Elpaca queues..." 'face 'bold))
        (elpaca-process-queues))

      ;; Reclaim orphaned Lisp objects generated by re-evaluation.
      (garbage-collect)

      (let ((elapsed-time (float-time (time-subtract (current-time) start-time))))
        (message "%s %s" (propertize "✓" 'face 'success) "Loaded")
        (message "%s" divider)
        (message "Completed in %s seconds"
                 (propertize (format "%.2f" elapsed-time) 'face 'warning))
        (message "")
        (message "%s" (propertize "RESTART MANDATE:" 'face 'error))
        (message "  %s Additions applied: variables, packages, keybindings."
                 (propertize "•" 'face 'font-lock-builtin-face))
        (message "  %s Deletions persist: packages and hooks remain in memory."
                 (propertize "•" 'face 'font-lock-builtin-face))
        (message "  %s Removed packages persist in var/elpaca/."
                 (propertize "•" 'face 'font-lock-builtin-face))
        (message "  %s Full restart required for structural teardown."
                 (propertize "•" 'face 'font-lock-builtin-face))
        (message "%s" divider)))))

(defun selected-region-or-symbol-at-point ()
  "Return the active region, or the symbol at point if inactive.
Provides a fallback for text operations lacking an explicit region."
  (if (region-active-p)
      (buffer-substring-no-properties (region-beginning) (region-end))
    (thing-at-point 'symbol t)))
#+end_src


** DONE Constants
System and Emacs version constants. Utilizes =user-uid= for reliable root privilege detection in containerized and sudo environments, and =version<= for semantic version compatibility across Emacs 29, 30, and 31.

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defconst sys/linuxp
  (eq system-type 'gnu/linux)
  "Non-nil if running on a GNU/Linux system.")

;; Utilizes `user-uid` instead of environment variables for reliable
;; root privilege detection in containerized and sudo environments.
(defconst sys/rootp
  (zerop (user-uid))
  "Non-nil if executing with root privileges.")

;; Semantic version checking via `version<=`. Prevents evaluation
;; failures on edge-case minor versions (e.g., Emacs 30.1).
(defconst emacs/>=29p
  (version<= "29" emacs-version)
  "Non-nil if Emacs version is 29.0 or above.")

(defconst emacs/>=29.2p
  (version<= "29.2" emacs-version)
  "Non-nil if Emacs version is 29.2 or above.")

(defconst emacs/>=30p
  (version<= "30" emacs-version)
  "Non-nil if Emacs version is 30.0 or above.")

(defconst emacs/>=31p
  (version<= "31" emacs-version)
  "Non-nil if Emacs version is 31.0 or above.")
#+end_src


** DONE Performance Tuning
Rendering, scrolling, and input-latency optimizations. Excludes obsolete Emacs 24 =bidi= variables to prevent rendering glitches. Mitigates clipboard I/O spam, GTK tooltip hangs, and main-thread blocking during rapid input or large file traversal.

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; Safe for Emacs 27+. Omits obsolete `bidi-display-reordering` to prevent rendering glitches.
;; LTR direction and BPA inhibition provide equivalent performance.
(setq-default bidi-paragraph-direction 'left-to-right
              bidi-inhibit-bpa t)

;; Disables GTK input methods on PGTK/Wayland builds to mitigate severe input latency.
;; Applied post-frame creation.
(when (fboundp 'pgtk-use-im-context)
  (add-hook 'after-make-frame-functions
            (lambda (frame)
              (with-selected-frame frame
                (pgtk-use-im-context nil)))))

;; Emacs 29+ long line optimizations. Prevents main-thread freezing on minified JSON
;; or large logs by disabling expensive redisplay features.
(setq-default long-line-threshold 1000
              large-hscroll-threshold 1000
              syntax-wholeline-max 1000)

;; Expanded undo limits for large refactoring operations on modern hardware.
(setq undo-limit 800000
      undo-strong-limit 1200000
      undo-outer-limit 120000000)

;; Scrolling and redisplay. Core variables initialized in early-init.el to prevent duplication.
(setq auto-window-vscroll nil
      highlight-nonselected-windows nil
      window-resize-pixelwise nil
      byte-compile-warnings '(not obsolete)
      native-comp-async-report-warnings-errors 'silent)

;; Halts the blink timer and prevents redisplay overhead.
;; `setq` merely alters variable state without stopping the timer.
(blink-cursor-mode -1)

;; Prevents OS-level DBus/X11 API calls on text highlight, eliminating selection micro-stutters.
(setq select-active-regions nil)

;; Skips expensive case-insensitive regex matching during `auto-mode-alist` evaluation.
(setq auto-mode-case-fold nil)

;; Defers syntax highlighting by 50ms to prevent font-lock and LSP tokens from blocking the main thread.
(setq jit-lock-defer-time 0.05)

;; Forces internal Emacs tooltips over the GTK DBus API to prevent UI hangs on hover.
(when (boundp 'x-gtk-use-system-tooltips)
  (setq x-gtk-use-system-tooltips nil))
#+end_src


** DONE Small Configs
Quality-of-life settings, safety guards, and core minor modes. Disables unused VC backends to prevent TRAMP I/O stutter, suppresses native OS dialog boxes to prevent daemon freezes, and protects the system clipboard from accidental overwrite during kill operations.

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; Restrict VCS backends to Git. Prevents TRAMP I/O stutter and SSH timeouts
;; caused by Emacs probing for bzr, svn, and hg on remote files.
(setq vc-handled-backends '(Git)
      vc-follow-symlinks t)

;; Suppress native OS dialog boxes. GTK/X11 dialogs freeze the Emacs daemon
;; when triggered from an emacsclient terminal session.
(setq use-dialog-box nil
      use-file-dialog nil)

;; Process and compilation safeguards.
(setq confirm-kill-processes nil
      compilation-always-kill t
      compilation-ask-about-save nil
      compilation-scroll-output t)

;; UI and input latency reductions.
(setq echo-keystrokes 0.1
      ad-redefinition-action 'accept)

;; Filesystem and buffer safety.
;; Disables lockfiles to prevent conflicts with modern file watchers (Webpack, Vite).
(setq-default create-lockfiles nil
              require-final-newline t)

;; Enable potentially dangerous commands without prompting.
(put 'erase-buffer 'disabled nil)

;; Core minor modes.
(delete-selection-mode 1)
(setq window-sides-vertical t)

;; Protect system clipboard. Saves external clipboard contents to the kill-ring
;; before Emacs overwrites them via kill/cut operations.
(setq save-interprogram-paste-before-kill t)

;; Routes GPG passphrase prompts to the native minibuffer, preventing external
;; GTK/Qt OS dialog freezes and preserving daemon stability on PGTK/Wayland.
(setq epg-pinentry-mode 'loopback)

;; Standardizes `q` to gracefully bury read-only dashboard buffers (*Packages*,
;; *Flycheck*, *Buffer List*, etc.) to match Vim/Emacs muscle memory.
(with-eval-after-load 'tabulated-list
  (define-key tabulated-list-mode-map "q" #'quit-window))


;; Fallback major-mode associations for extensions lacking native Emacs support.
(dolist (pair '(("\\.in\\'" . text-mode)
                ("\\.out\\'" . text-mode)
                ("\\.args\\'" . text-mode)
                ("\\.bb\\'" . shell-script-mode)
                ("\\.bbclass\\'" . shell-script-mode)
                ("\\.Rmd\\'" . markdown-mode)))
  (add-to-list 'auto-mode-alist pair))
#+end_src




** DONE Garbage Collector
Manages garbage collection via =gcmh= idle timers. Elevates the GC threshold during active input and triggers background collection during idle periods. Disables =gcmh= on experimental MPS Incremental GC (=igc=) builds and utilizes =after-focus-change-function= for focus-out memory reclamation.

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package gcmh
  ;; Disabled on experimental MPS Incremental GC (igc) builds.
  ;; gcmh targets the legacy stop-the-world GC and conflicts with igc.
  :unless (fboundp 'igc-info)
  :hook (elpaca-after-init . gcmh-mode)
  :init
  (setq gcmh-idle-delay 'auto
        gcmh-auto-idle-delay-factor 10
        ;; 128MB baseline threshold to prevent LSP-induced GC stutters.
        gcmh-high-cons-threshold (* 128 1024 1024)))

;; Triggers GC on frame focus loss. Replaces obsolete `focus-out-hook`.
(add-function :after after-focus-change-function
              (lambda ()
                (unless (frame-focus-state)
                  (garbage-collect))))
#+end_src


** DONE UTF-8 Coding System
Enforces UTF-8 across all I/O boundaries. Excludes the selection coding system on Windows to preserve native =utf-16-le= clipboard integration.

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; Core language environment and internal boundaries.
(set-language-environment "UTF-8")
(set-default-coding-systems 'utf-8)
(set-terminal-coding-system 'utf-8)
(set-keyboard-coding-system 'utf-8)
(setq locale-coding-system 'utf-8)

;; Preserves native utf-16-le clipboard integration on Windows.
;; Forcing utf-8 breaks non-ASCII copy/paste on Win32.
(unless (eq system-type 'windows-nt)
  (set-selection-coding-system 'utf-8))

;; Prioritizes UTF-8 for unknown file encodings.
(prefer-coding-system 'utf-8)
#+end_src


** DONE Session Management
Persists cursor positions, recent files, and minibuffer history across sessions. Excludes TRAMP paths and unmounted symlinks to prevent I/O hangs. Utilizes a lazy cache for =org-agenda-files= to prevent filesystem thrashing, and injects a background timer to flush the =recentf= cache periodically, mitigating data loss in daemon environments.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package saveplace
  :ensure nil
  :custom
  ;; Prevents startup hangs from restoring point in offline/unmounted volumes.
  (save-place-forget-unreadable-files t)
  :config
  (save-place-mode 1))

(use-package recentf
  :ensure nil
  :init
  (setq recentf-max-saved-items 300
        recentf-exclude
        '("\\.?cache" ".cask" "url" "COMMIT_EDITMSG\\'" "bookmarks"
          "\\.\\(?:gz\\|gif\\|svg\\|png\\|jpe?g\\|bmp\\|xpm\\)$"
          "\\.?ido\\.last$" "\\.revive$" "/G?TAGS$" "/.elfeed/"
          "^/tmp/" "^/var/folders/.+$" "/persp-confs/"))
  ;; Custom predicate functions must exist in memory before mode activation.
  (defvar ar/recentf-org-agenda-cache nil
    "Cached list of resolved org-agenda file paths.")
  (defun ar/update-recentf-org-agenda-cache ()
    "Update the recentf exclusion cache for org-agenda files."
    (when (bound-and-true-p org-agenda-files)
      (setq ar/recentf-org-agenda-cache
            (mapcar #'file-truename
                    (seq-filter #'file-exists-p (org-agenda-files t))))))
  (defun ar/exclude-org-agenda-files-p (file)
    "Return t if FILE is an org-agenda file, using the cached list."
    (and (not (file-remote-p file))
         (string-suffix-p ".org" file t)
         (when ar/recentf-org-agenda-cache
           (member (file-truename file) ar/recentf-org-agenda-cache))))
  :config
  (recentf-mode 1)
  (push (expand-file-name recentf-save-file) recentf-exclude)
  ;; Exclude no-littering housekeeping files.
  (add-to-list 'recentf-exclude (recentf-expand-file-name no-littering-var-directory))
  (add-to-list 'recentf-exclude (recentf-expand-file-name no-littering-etc-directory))
  ;; Exclude TRAMP/remote paths to prevent SSH timeouts during cleanup.
  (add-to-list 'recentf-exclude #'file-remote-p)
  ;; Remove `file-truename` to prevent I/O hangs on unmounted symlink targets.
  (setq recentf-filename-handlers '(abbreviate-file-name))
  ;; Daemon-safe periodic flush (every 5 minutes) to prevent data loss
  ;; when Emacs daemon crashes or receives SIGKILL without triggering `kill-emacs-hook`.
  (run-at-time 300 300 #'recentf-save-list)
  (add-to-list 'recentf-exclude #'ar/exclude-org-agenda-files-p)
  (add-hook 'org-mode-hook #'ar/update-recentf-org-agenda-cache)
  (run-with-idle-timer 30 t #'ar/update-recentf-org-agenda-cache))

(use-package savehist
  :ensure nil
  :init
  (setq enable-recursive-minibuffers t
        history-length 1000
        savehist-additional-variables '(global-mark-ring
                                        search-ring
                                        regexp-search-ring
                                        extended-command-history)
        savehist-autosave-interval 300)
  :config
  (savehist-mode 1))
#+end_src



** DONE Misc
Configures core =simple.el= behaviors, modeline indicators, whitespace management, and text navigation.

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package simple
  :ensure nil
  :hook ((text-mode . visual-line-mode))
  :init
  ;; Enables column number display in the modeline.
  (column-number-mode 1)

  ;; Core editing behaviors: logical line movement, EOL tracking, and mark ring repetition.
  (setq kill-whole-line t
        line-move-visual nil
        track-eol t
        set-mark-command-repeat-pop t)

  ;; Zero-overhead trailing space highlighting. Bypasses `whitespace-mode`'s
  ;; heavy font-lock injection to prevent Tree-sitter conflicts in Emacs 30/31.
  (setq-default show-trailing-whitespace t)
  ;; Disable in prose buffers where trailing spaces are structurally irrelevant.
  (dolist (mode '(org-mode markdown-mode text-mode))
    (add-hook (intern (format "%s-hook" mode))
              (lambda () (setq-local show-trailing-whitespace nil))))

  ;; Replaces verbose yes/no prompts with single-character y/n inputs (Emacs 28+).
  (setq use-short-answers t)

  ;; Default buffer states: text mode, 80-column fill, 4-space indentation.
  (setq-default major-mode 'text-mode
                fill-column 80
                tab-width 4
                indent-tabs-mode nil)

  ;; Silences audible and visual bells.
  (setq visible-bell nil
        ring-bell-function #'ignore)

  ;; Suppresses echo-area warnings for buffer boundaries.
  (setq command-error-function
        (lambda (data context caller)
          (when (not (memq (car data) '(beginning-of-buffer end-of-buffer)))
            (command-error-default-function data context caller))))

  ;; Resolves duplicate buffer names using full directory paths.
  (setq uniquify-buffer-name-style 'forward)

  ;; Filesystem and auto-fill safeguards. Disables legacy backups and auto-save.
  (setq delete-by-moving-to-trash t
        make-backup-files nil
        auto-save-default nil
        ;; Regex for auto-filling blockquotes, bullets, and numbered lists.
        adaptive-fill-regexp "[ \t]*\\(>[ \t]*\\|[-–—]\\|[•‣*]\\|[0-9]+[.)]\\)[ \t]*"
        adaptive-fill-first-line-regexp "^* *$"
        ;; Sentence boundary regex. Utilizes explicit newline escape to prevent tangling corruption.
        sentence-end "\\([。！？]\\|……\\|[.?!][]\"')}]*\\($\\|[ \t]\\)\\)[ \t\n]*"
        sentence-end-double-space nil
        word-wrap-by-category t))
#+end_src

* DONE Vim Emulation
** DONE Undo Fu
Provides linear undo and redo actions with persistent history across Emacs sessions.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package undo-fu
  :defer t)
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package undo-fu-session
  :defer t
  :custom
  ;; Route persistent undo history to the no-littering var directory.
  (undo-fu-session-directory (no-littering-expand-var-file-name "undo-fu-session/"))
  ;; Align disk footprint with undo-fu's linear paradigm; ignore branching history.
  (undo-fu-session-linear t)
  ;; Cull oldest session files to prevent infinite filesystem bloat and I/O stutter.
  (undo-fu-session-file-limit 500)
  ;; Exclude transient Git files to prevent metadata leakage and I/O errors.
  (undo-fu-session-incompatible-files
   '("/COMMIT_EDITMSG\\'"
     "/git-rebase-todo\\'"))
  ;; Exclude heavy, non-text UI, VCS, and terminal buffers from session serialization.
  (undo-fu-session-incompatible-major-modes
   '(magit-status-mode
     magit-log-mode
     magit-diff-mode
     magit-revision-mode
     git-commit-mode
     dired-mode
     dirvish-mode
     treemacs-mode
     dashboard-mode
     eshell-mode
     vterm-mode
     term-mode
     eat-mode
     ghostel-mode
     compilation-mode
     grep-mode
     occur-mode
     org-agenda-mode
     pdf-view-mode
     help-mode
     helpful-mode
     Info-mode
     special-mode))
  :config
  ;; Conditional compression: prefer zstd for near-instantaneous I/O, fallback to gzip.
  (setq undo-fu-session-compression
        (cond ((executable-find "zstd") 'zst)
              ((executable-find "gzip") 'gz)
              (t nil)))
  ;; Bulletproof path hashing: utilizes stable `secure-hash` primitive to bypass OS
  ;; 255-char limits while appending the truncated base filename for human readability.
  (defun ar/undo-fu-session-hash-path (file ext)
    "Safely hash FILE to bypass OS 255-char limits, appending EXT."
    (let* ((base (file-name-nondirectory file))
           (hash (substring (secure-hash 'sha1 file) 0 16)))
      (expand-file-name (concat hash "-" base ext) undo-fu-session-directory)))
  (setq undo-fu-session-make-file-name-function #'ar/undo-fu-session-hash-path)
  ;; Activate global minor mode directly in :config per Elpaca load-order physics.
  (undo-fu-session-global-mode 1))
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package evil
  :ensure (:wait t)
  :demand t
  :init
  ;; Core Settings (Must be set before load)
  (setq evil-want-integration t
        evil-want-keybinding nil
        evil-want-minibuffer t
        evil-mode-line-format nil
        evil-kbd-macro-suppress-motion-error t
        evil-symbol-word-search t
        evil-kill-on-visual-paste nil)
  ;; Extended Keybindings Configuration
  (setq evil-want-C-g-bindings t
        evil-want-C-i-jump nil
        evil-want-C-u-scroll t
        evil-want-C-u-delete t
        evil-want-C-w-delete t
        evil-want-Y-yank-to-eol t
        evil-want-abbrev-expand-on-insert-exit nil
        evil-respect-visual-line-mode nil
        ;; Load-order fix: Must be in :init to register before keymap generation.
        evil-want-C-h-delete t)
  ;; State-Dependent Cursor Shapes
  (setq evil-normal-state-cursor '(box)
        evil-insert-state-cursor '(bar)
        evil-visual-state-cursor '(hollow)
        evil-replace-state-cursor '(underline))
  :custom
  (evil-ex-visual-char-range t)
  (evil-ex-search-vim-style-regexp t)
  (evil-split-window-below t)
  (evil-vsplit-window-right t)
  (evil-echo-state nil)
  (evil-move-cursor-back nil)
  (evil-v$-excludes-newline t)
  (evil-want-fine-undo t)
  ;; Vim Parity: Prevent cursor from moving past EOL.
  (evil-move-beyond-eol nil)
  (evil-search-wrap t)
  (select-enable-clipboard t)
  (evil-visual-update-x-selection-p nil)
  ;; Search Improvements
  (evil-ex-search-case 'smart)
  (evil-ex-search-incremental t)
  (evil-ex-search-highlight-all t)
  :config
  (evil-mode 1)
  (evil-select-search-module 'evil-search-module 'evil-search)
  (setq-default evil-shift-width tab-width)
  ;; Ex & Search History Cycling (Arrow Keys & Emacs bindings)
  (dolist (keymap (list evil-ex-completion-map evil-ex-search-keymap))
    (define-key keymap (kbd "<up>") #'previous-history-element)
    (define-key keymap (kbd "<down>") #'next-history-element)
    (define-key keymap (kbd "C-p") #'previous-history-element)
    (define-key keymap (kbd "C-n") #'next-history-element))
  ;; Better Initial States
  (evil-set-initial-state 'messages-buffer-mode 'normal)
  (evil-set-initial-state 'dashboard-mode 'normal))
#+end_src

** DONE Smart Comment Continuation
Intercepts Evil's =o= and =O= operators to continue comment lines natively. Utilizes bounded O(1) line searches to verify comment presence, ensure correct prefix propagation, and guarantee precise cursor placement.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defcustom ar/evil-want-o/O-to-continue-comments t
  "If non-nil, o/O keys will continue comment lines."
  :type 'boolean
  :group 'evil)

(defun ar/evil-open-below-smart ()
  "Open line below, continuing comments if applicable."
  (interactive)
  (if (and ar/evil-want-o/O-to-continue-comments
           comment-start-skip
           (save-excursion
             (beginning-of-line)
             (re-search-forward comment-start-skip (line-end-position) t)))
      (progn
        (end-of-line)
        (comment-indent-new-line)
        (evil-insert-state))
    (evil-open-below (prefix-numeric-value current-prefix-arg))))

(defun ar/evil-open-above-smart ()
  "Open line above, continuing comments if applicable."
  (interactive)
  (if (and ar/evil-want-o/O-to-continue-comments
           comment-start-skip
           (save-excursion
             (beginning-of-line)
             (re-search-forward comment-start-skip (line-end-position) t)))
      (progn
        (beginning-of-line)
        (re-search-forward comment-start-skip (line-end-position) t)
        (comment-indent-new-line)
        (forward-line -1)
        (indent-according-to-mode)
        ;; Push cursor past the delimiter on the newly created line.
        (re-search-forward comment-start-skip (line-end-position) t)
        (evil-insert-state))
    (evil-open-above (prefix-numeric-value current-prefix-arg))))
#+end_src

  :ensure (:wait t)

  :after evil

  (general-create-definer ar/global-leader
    :states '(normal insert visual emacs)
    :prefix "SPC"
    :global-prefix "C-SPC")

  ;; Establishes the local leader prefix (SPC m) for major-mode-specific commands.
  (general-create-definer ar/local-leader
    :states '(normal insert visual emacs)
    :prefix "SPC m"
    :global-prefix "C-SPC m"))

(ar/global-leader
 "SPC" '(execute-extended-command :wk "M-x")

** DONE Child Frame
Establishes the foundational child frame API and global border theming. Configures native Emacs 28+ border faces and applies fringe isolation for downstream UI packages.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package posframe
  :defer t
  :custom-face
  ;; Themes the native Emacs 28+ child frame border to inherit the active region face.
  (child-frame-border ((t (:inherit region))))
  :config
  ;; Prevents the parent frame's fringe background from bleeding into the child frame.
  ;; Guarded by `frame-live-p` to prevent redisplay crashes during daemon teardown.
  (with-no-warnings
    (defun ar/posframe--isolate-fringe (&rest _)
      (when (and (boundp 'posframe--frame)
                 (frame-live-p posframe--frame))
        (set-face-background 'fringe nil posframe--frame)))
    (advice-add #'posframe--create-posframe :after #'ar/posframe--isolate-fringe))
  ;; Mathematically corrected poshandler for near-bottom centering.
  ;; Subtracts the child frame's height to prevent bottom-edge clipping.
  (defun ar/posframe-poshandler-frame-center-near-bottom (info)
    (let ((parent-width (plist-get info :parent-frame-width))
          (parent-height (plist-get info :parent-frame-height))
          (posframe-width (plist-get info :posframe-width))
          (posframe-height (plist-get info :posframe-height))
          (font-height (plist-get info :font-height)))
      (cons (/ (- parent-width posframe-width) 2)
            (- parent-height posframe-height (* 2 font-height))))))
#+end_src

** DONE Fonts
Configures global typography, baseline monospaced faces, fontset fallback registration, and vertical line centering.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defun ar/setup-fonts ()
  "Apply global typography, baseline faces, and fontset fallbacks to the current frame."
  (set-face-attribute 'default nil :font "JetBrainsMono Nerd Font" :height 125 :weight 'medium)
  (set-face-attribute 'fixed-pitch nil :font "JetBrainsMono Nerd Font" :height 125 :weight 'medium)
  ;; Maps variable-pitch to monospaced to preserve Org-mode table alignment.
  (set-face-attribute 'variable-pitch nil :font "JetBrainsMono Nerd Font" :height 125 :weight 'medium)
  ;; Pre-register Noto Sans for Unicode blocks used by org-modern-replace-stars.
   (set-fontset-font t '(#x25A0 . #x25FF) "Noto Sans")  ; Geometric Shapes (◉ ○ ◇ ▷)
  (set-fontset-font t '(#x2600 . #x26FF) "Noto Sans")  ; Miscellaneous Symbols
  (set-fontset-font t '(#x2700 . #x27BF) "Noto Sans")) ; Dingbats (✸ ✿ ✤ ✜)

;; Applies fonts immediately for standard GUI sessions.
(unless noninteractive
  (ar/setup-fonts))

;; Daemon-safe hook: applies fonts to GUI frames spawned by emacsclient.
(add-hook 'server-after-make-frame-hook #'ar/setup-fonts)

;; Vertically centers org-modern badges (TODO, tags, priorities) against text baseline.
(setq-default line-spacing '(2 . 2))

;; Maximizes syntax highlighting decoration across all programming buffers.
(setq font-lock-maximum-decoration t)
#+end_src

** DONE Prettify Symbols & Ligatures
Delegates operator ligatures to HarfBuzz via ligature.el and reserves Elisp substitution strictly for semantic keywords.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package ligature
  :demand t
  :config
  ;; Maps ASCII sequences to JetBrains Mono's native OpenType GSUB ligatures.
  ;; Excludes equality/inequality operators and ellipses to prevent visual ambiguity.
  (ligature-set-ligatures 'prog-mode
                          '("=>" "->" "->>" "-->" "<-" "<--" "<=" ">=" ">>" "<<"
                            "&&" "||" "::" ":=" "~~" "**" "www" "##" "###" "####"
                            "<!--" "|>" "<|" "</" "/>" ">>=" "<<="))
  (global-ligature-mode t))

;; Establishes the baseline alist for keywords lacking native OpenType ligatures.
(setq-default prettify-symbols-alist '(("lambda" . ?λ)))

;; Reveals raw text when the cursor touches the symbol boundary for precise editing.
(setq prettify-symbols-unprettify-at-point 'right-edge)

;; Activates globally across prog-mode, text-mode, and conf-mode derivatives.
(global-prettify-symbols-mode 1)
#+end_src

(use-package ghostel
  :defer t
  :commands (ghostel ghostel-project)
  :bind (:map ghostel-semi-char-mode-map
         ;; Map M-p/M-n to terminal history navigation.
         ("M-p" . (lambda () (interactive) (ghostel-send-key "p" "ctrl")))
         ("M-n" . (lambda () (interactive) (ghostel-send-key "n" "ctrl")))
         ("M-<backspace>" . ghostel-backward-kill-word))
  :config
  (require 'seq)
  ;; Route buffers to a dedicated 30% bottom side-window.
  (add-to-list 'display-buffer-alist
               '((derived-mode . ghostel-mode)
                 (display-buffer-in-side-window)
                 (side . bottom)
                 (window-height . 0.3)
                 (window-parameters (no-delete-other-windows . t))))
  ;; Whitelist Magit for OSC 52;e shell evaluation.
  (add-to-list 'ghostel-eval-cmds '("magit-status-setup-buffer" magit-status-setup-buffer))
  ;; TODO: Integrate natively with  workflow management section
  (add-to-list 'project-switch-commands '(ghostel-project "Ghostel") t)
  (add-to-list 'project-switch-commands '(ghostel-project-list-buffers "Ghostel buffers") t)
  ;; Synchronize C-k kill-ring behavior with terminal line deletion.
  (defun ar/ghostel-send-C-k-and-kill ()
    "Send `C-k' to ghostel and save to kill-ring."
    (interactive)
    (copy-region-as-kill (point) (line-end-position))
    (ghostel-send-key "k" "ctrl"))
  (define-key ghostel-semi-char-mode-map (kbd "C-k") #'ar/ghostel-send-C-k-and-kill)
  ;; Strip DIRENV_ variables to enforce local .envrc evaluation.
  (add-hook 'ghostel-pre-spawn-hook
            (lambda ()
              (setq process-environment
                    (seq-remove (lambda (env) (string-prefix-p "DIRENV_" env))
                                process-environment))))
  ;; Enable automatic TRAMP shell integration via temporary SCP transfers.
  (setq ghostel-tramp-shell-integration t)
  ;; TODO: Configure OS-level Zsh aliases for ghostel_cmd IDE synergy.
  ;; TODO: Configure OS-level Tmux for xterm-ghostty true-color passthrough.
  )

;; State-aware toggle for the persistent bottom terminal drawer.
(defun ar/ghostel-toggle ()
  "Toggle the Ghostel terminal drawer at the bottom of the frame."
  (interactive)
  ;; TODO: Restrict buffer querying to persp-mode/projectile boundaries.
  (let* ((buf (seq-find (lambda (b)
                          (with-current-buffer b (derived-mode-p 'ghostel-mode)))
                        (buffer-list)))
         (win (and buf (get-buffer-window buf))))
    (cond
     ((and win (eq win (selected-window))) (delete-window win))
     (win (select-window win))
     (buf (pop-to-buffer buf))
     (t (call-interactively #'ghostel)))))

;; Synchronizes the terminal cursor with Emacs point during Evil state transitions.
(use-package evil-ghostel
  :after (ghostel evil)
  :hook (ghostel-mode . evil-ghostel-mode))

;; Routes Eshell visual commands to dedicated Ghostel buffers for proper TTY rendering.
(use-package ghostel-eshell
  :ensure nil
  :after ghostel
  :hook (eshell-load . ghostel-eshell-visual-command-mode))

;; Replaces standard compilation buffers with live TTY-backed Ghostel sessions.
(use-package ghostel-compile
  :ensure nil
  :after ghostel
  :hook (elpaca-after-init . ghostel-compile-global-mode))

;; Upgrades Comint and REPL output with libghostty-vt ANSI and OSC parsing fidelity.

   '("top" "htop" "btop" "less" "more" "vim" "vi" "nvim" "nano" "emacs"
     "ssh" "tail" "watch" "ncdu" "ranger" "tmux" "screen" "zellij"
     "python" "ipython" "node" "ruby" "irb" "ncmpcpp" "lazygit"))
  (eshell-visual-subcommands
   '(("git" "log" "diff" "show" "help")
     ("docker" "run" "exec" "logs" "attach")
     ("podman" "run" "exec" "logs" "attach")))
  (eshell-visual-options
   '(("git" "--help" "--paginate")))
  (eshell-prefer-lisp-functions t)
  (eshell-prefer-lisp-variables t)
  :config
  (require 'seq)
  (setenv "PAGER" "cat")
  (add-to-list 'eshell-modules-list 'eshell-tramp)
  ;; Prevent interactive alias editor from overwriting Elisp definitions.
  (advice-add #'eshell-write-aliases-list :override #'ignore)
  ;; Imenu prompt jumping for `consult-imenu` synergy.
  (add-hook 'eshell-mode-hook
            (lambda ()
              (setq-local imenu-generic-expression
                          `((,(propertize "λ" 'face 'eshell-prompt)
                             ,(concat eshell-prompt-regexp "\\(.*\\)") 1)))))
  ;; Undo history protection and read-only output enforcement.
  (add-hook 'eshell-pre-command-hook #'buffer-disable-undo)
  (add-hook 'eshell-post-command-hook
            (lambda ()
              (buffer-enable-undo (current-buffer))
              (setq buffer-undo-list nil)))
  (add-hook 'eshell-pre-command-hook
            (lambda ()
              (when (and eshell-last-input-start eshell-last-input-end)
                (add-text-properties eshell-last-input-start
                                     (1- eshell-last-input-end)
                                     '(read-only t)))))
  (add-hook 'eshell-post-command-hook
            (lambda ()
              (when (and eshell-last-input-end eshell-last-output-start)
                (add-text-properties eshell-last-input-end
                                     eshell-last-output-start
                                     '(read-only t)))))
  ;; Inject unwrapped cape-file for unquoted path completion.
  (add-hook 'eshell-mode-hook
            (lambda ()
              (add-hook 'completion-at-point-functions #'cape-file nil t)))
  ;; State-aware 33% bottom drawer toggle with sole-window guard.
  (defun ar/eshell-toggle ()
    "Toggle eshell popup window at the bottom of the frame."
    (interactive)
    (let* ((eshell-buffer-name "*eshell*")
           (eshell-buffer (get-buffer eshell-buffer-name))
           (eshell-window (and eshell-buffer (get-buffer-window eshell-buffer))))
      (if eshell-window
          (if (one-window-p) (bury-buffer) (delete-window eshell-window))
        (let ((display-buffer-alist
               '(("\\*eshell\\*"
                  (display-buffer-in-side-window)
                  (side . bottom)
                  (window-height . 0.33)
                  (window-parameters (no-delete-other-windows . t))))))
          (if eshell-buffer
              (pop-to-buffer eshell-buffer)
            (eshell))))))
  ;; Spawn fully initialized new eshell instance.
  (defun ar/eshell-new ()
    "Create a new eshell buffer."
    (interactive)
    (eshell t))
  ;; Safe scrollback clearing without destroying prompt state.
  (with-eval-after-load 'em-basic
    (defalias 'eshell/clear #'eshell/clear-scrollback))
  ;; Optimized Git branch helper using Magit/VC backends to prevent main-thread blocking.
  (defun ar/eshell-git-branch ()
    "Retrieve current git branch via Magit or VC backend."
    (when (and (not (file-remote-p default-directory))
               (vc-git-root default-directory))
      (let ((branch (or (and (fboundp 'magit-get-current-branch)
                             (magit-get-current-branch))
                        (ignore-errors (vc-git--symbolic-ref default-directory)))))
        (when branch
          (concat " " (propertize (concat "(" branch ")")
                                  'face '(:foreground "#7aa2f7")))))))
  ;; Custom prompt with O(1) Git branch resolution.
  (setq eshell-prompt-function
        (lambda ()
          (concat
           (propertize (abbreviate-file-name (eshell/pwd))
                       'face '(:foreground "#bb9af7"))
           (ar/eshell-git-branch)
           (propertize (if (zerop (user-uid)) " # " " $ ")
                       'face '(:foreground "#9ece6a" :weight bold)))))
  (setq eshell-prompt-regexp "^[^#$\n]* [#$] ")
  ;; Aliases
  (defalias 'eshell/ff #'find-file)
  (defalias 'eshell/fo #'find-file-other-window)
  (defalias 'eshell/d #'dired)
  (defalias 'eshell/e #'find-file)
  (defalias 'eshell/eh #'find-file-other-window)
  (defalias 'eshell/v #'view-file)
  (defalias 'eshell/l #'eshell/ls)
  (defalias 'eshell/ll #'eshell/ls)
  (defalias 'eshell/q #'eshell/exit)
  (defalias 'eshell/gst #'magit-status))

  :hook (eshell-mode . eshell-syntax-highlighting-mode)

  (defun ar/eshell-filter-history-from-highlighting-a (&rest _)
    (memq this-command '(eshell-previous-matching-input-from-input
                         eshell-next-matching-input-from-input)))
  (advice-add #'eshell-syntax-highlighting--enable-highlighting
              :before-until #'ar/eshell-filter-history-from-highlighting-a)
  (add-hook 'eshell-syntax-highlighting-elisp-buffer-setup-hook

                (add-hook 'pre-command-hook

                                                       eshell-next-matching-input-from-input))
                              (eshell-syntax-highlighting--enable-highlighting)))

(use-package pcmpl-args

(use-package eshell-did-you-mean

** DONE Smooth Scrolling
Delegates hardware input to =ultra-scroll= and keyboard interpolation to =good-scroll=. Utilizes defensive safeguards to prevent boundary crashes and input hijacking.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; Core viewport stability and input latency variables.
(setq hscroll-step 1
      hscroll-margin 2
      scroll-step 1
      scroll-margin 0
      scroll-conservatively 100000
      scroll-preserve-screen-position t
      mouse-wheel-scroll-amount-horizontal 1
      mouse-wheel-progressive-speed nil)

;; Pixel-precision engine for hardware input devices.
(use-package ultra-scroll
  :if (fboundp 'pixel-scroll-precision-mode)
  :hook (elpaca-after-init . ultra-scroll-mode)
  :config
  ;; Suspend expensive minor modes during high-speed scrolling to prevent redisplay stutter.
  (add-hook 'ultra-scroll-hide-functions #'hl-todo-mode)
  (add-hook 'ultra-scroll-hide-functions #'diff-hl-flydiff-mode)
  (add-hook 'ultra-scroll-hide-functions #'jit-lock-mode)
  (add-hook 'ultra-scroll-hide-functions #'good-scroll-mode))

;; Interpolation engine for keyboard-driven viewport movements.
(use-package good-scroll
  :if (fboundp 'pixel-scroll-precision-mode)
  :hook (elpaca-after-init . good-scroll-mode)
  :config
  ;; Neuter mouse-wheel interception to prevent conflict with ultra-scroll.
  (add-hook 'good-scroll-mode-hook
            (lambda ()
              (when good-scroll-mode
                (setq mwheel-scroll-up-function #'scroll-up
                      mwheel-scroll-down-function #'scroll-down))))

  ;; Clamp boundary calculations to prevent args-out-of-range errors in narrowed buffers.
  (defun ar/good-scroll--point-at-top-p-safe ()
    (save-restriction
      (widen)
      (<= (line-number-at-pos (max (point) (point-min)) t)
          (1+ (line-number-at-pos (min (window-start) (point-max)) t)))))
  (advice-add #'good-scroll--point-at-top-p :override #'ar/good-scroll--point-at-top-p-safe)

  ;; Translate line counts to exact pixel heights for page scrolling.
  (defun ar/good-scroll--convert-line-to-step (line)
    (cond ((integerp line) (* line (line-pixel-height)))
          ((or (null line) (memq '- line))
           (- (good-scroll--window-usable-height)
              (* next-screen-context-lines (line-pixel-height))))
          ((line-pixel-height))))

  ;; Route scroll-up commands through the interpolation engine.
  (defun ar/good-scroll--scroll-up-around (fn &optional arg)
    (if good-scroll-mode
        (good-scroll-move (ar/good-scroll--convert-line-to-step arg))
      (funcall fn arg)))
  (advice-add #'scroll-up :around #'ar/good-scroll--scroll-up-around)

  ;; Route scroll-down commands through the interpolation engine.
  (defun ar/good-scroll--scroll-down-around (fn &optional arg)
    (if good-scroll-mode
        (good-scroll-move (- (ar/good-scroll--convert-line-to-step arg)))
      (funcall fn arg)))
  (advice-add #'scroll-down :around #'ar/good-scroll--scroll-down-around))
#+end_src

(use-package treesit
  :ensure nil
  :if (treesit-available-p)
  :mode (("\\.tsx\\'" . tsx-ts-mode))
  :init
  ;; Stores downloaded language definitions in the designated cache directory.
  (defvar ar/treesit-grammar-dir
    (no-littering-expand-var-file-name "tree-sitter/")
    "Target directory for compiled tree-sitter grammars.")
  ;; Ensures the directory exists before downloading definitions.
  (unless (file-exists-p ar/treesit-grammar-dir)

  :custom
  ;; Directs the grammar loader to the designated cache directory.
  (treesit-extra-load-path (list ar/treesit-grammar-dir))
  ;; Enables automatic downloading of missing language definitions.
  (treesit-auto-install-grammar t)
  ;; Automatically uses advanced modes for supported file types.

  ;; Registers official grammar repositories for automatic downloading.

                    (bash "https://github.com/tree-sitter/tree-sitter-bash")

                    (make "https://github.com/tree-sitter-grammars/tree-sitter-make")
                    (awk "https://github.com/Beaglefoot/tree-sitter-awk")
                    (org "https://github.com/milisims/tree-sitter-org")

                    (tsx "https://github.com/tree-sitter/tree-sitter-typescript" "master" "tsx/src")))
    (setf (alist-get (car source) treesit-language-source-alist) (cdr source))))

** DONE Electric Pair
Native auto-pairing, O(1) prose formatting, and AST-aware re-indentation.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package elec-pair
  :ensure nil
  :init
  ;; Conservative inhibition prevents pairing in unbalanced buffers and complex strings.
  (setq electric-pair-inhibit-predicate #'electric-pair-conservative-inhibit
        electric-pair-skip-whitespace t
        electric-pair-delete-adjacent-pairs t)
  :config
  (electric-pair-mode 1)
  ;; O(1) multi-character pair injection for prose formatting.
  ;; Strictly inhibited inside source blocks and inline code.
  (defun ar/elec-pair-multi-char-prose ()
    (when (and (derived-mode-p 'text-mode 'org-mode 'markdown-mode)
               (not (and (derived-mode-p 'org-mode) (org-in-src-block-p)))
               (not (and (derived-mode-p 'markdown-mode)
                         (or (and (fboundp 'markdown-code-block-at-point-p)
                                  (markdown-code-block-at-point-p))
                             (and (fboundp 'markdown-inline-code-at-point-p)
                                  (markdown-inline-code-at-point-p))))))
      (let* ((c1 (char-before))
             (c2 (char-before (1- (point))))
             (before-pair (char-before (- (point) 2)))
             (after-pair (char-after)))
        (when (and c1 c2 (eq c1 c2)
                   (memq c1 '(?* ?= ?_ ?~))
                   ;; Prevent triggering if appending to existing identical punctuation (e.g. ***)
                   (not (eq before-pair c1))
                   (not (eq after-pair c1))
                   ;; Only trigger in "empty" space or before words, not after words
                   (or (null before-pair) (not (memq (char-syntax before-pair) '(?w ?_)))))
          (insert (string c1 c1))
          (backward-char 2)))))
  (add-hook 'post-self-insert-hook #'ar/elec-pair-multi-char-prose))

;; Native electric indentation and "Electric Words" engine.
(use-package electric
  :ensure nil
  :init
  ;; Force newline and backspace to trigger electric indentation.
  ;; ?\^? enables "Smart Backspace" (dedenting by full indentation level)
  ;; in modes like Python and C when pressing backspace in leading whitespace.
  (setq-default electric-indent-chars (append '(?\n ?\^?) electric-indent-chars))
  :config
  (defcustom ar/electric-indent-words '("else" "elif" "elseif" "catch" "finally" "except")
    "Words that trigger immediate re-indentation when typed at EOL."
    :type '(repeat string)
    :group 'electric)

  (defun ar/electric-indent-words-fn (_char)
    "Force re-indentation if an electric word is typed at the end of the line.
Strictly confined to `prog-mode' derivatives to prevent prose collision."
    (when (and (derived-mode-p 'prog-mode)
               (eolp)
               ar/electric-indent-words)
      (save-excursion
        (backward-word)
        (looking-at-p (concat "\\<" (regexp-opt ar/electric-indent-words) "\\>")))))

  (add-hook 'electric-indent-functions #'ar/electric-indent-words-fn))
#+end_src


** DONE Evil Surround
Provides retro-active Vim-style wrapping via =ys=, =cs=, and =ds= operators. Integrates =evil-embrace= to extend the base alist with regex-aware, multi-character, and asymmetric structural pairs for prose and LaTeX environments.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package evil-surround
  :after evil
  :config
  ;; Enable global Vim-style surround operators.
  (global-evil-surround-mode 1))

(use-package evil-embrace
  :after evil-surround
  :config
  ;; Merge regex-aware structural engine into evil-surround operators.
  (evil-embrace-enable-evil-surround-integration)

  ;; Native Org-mode emphasis pairs (*, /, _, =, ~) with regex boundaries.
  (add-hook 'org-mode-hook #'embrace-org-mode-hook)

  ;; Native LaTeX pairs ($, \verb). Deep environments handled downstream by evil-tex.
  (add-hook 'LaTeX-mode-hook #'embrace-LaTeX-mode-hook)

  ;; Markdown/GFM pair injection (embrace.el lacks a native markdown hook).
  (defun ar/embrace-markdown-setup ()
    "Inject markdown emphasis and code pairs into embrace."
    (embrace-add-pair ?* "*" "*")
    (embrace-add-pair ?_ "_" "_")
    (embrace-add-pair ?` "`" "`"))
  (add-hook 'markdown-mode-hook #'ar/embrace-markdown-setup)
  (add-hook 'gfm-mode-hook #'ar/embrace-markdown-setup))

** DONE Delete Pair
Configures the native =delete-pair= engine and provides an O(1) DWIM wrapper for adjacent, string, and nested pair deletion, strictly confined to programming modes.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; Emacs 28+: Visual blink delay before deletion.
(setq delete-pair-blink-delay 0.1)

;; Emacs 31+: Push mark before deletion for inner-text recovery.
(when (boundp 'delete-pair-push-mark)
  (setq delete-pair-push-mark t))

(defun ar/delete-pair-dwim ()
  "Intelligently delete the pair surrounding or preceding the cursor.
Strictly confined to `prog-mode' to prevent structural destruction in prose."
  (interactive)
  (unless (derived-mode-p 'prog-mode)
    (user-error "Delete Pair DWIM restricted to prog-mode; use `ds` in prose"))
  (let ((prev (char-before))
        (next (char-after))
        (ppss (syntax-ppss)))
    (cond
     ;; Delete preceding closing delimiter or quote.
     ((and prev (memq (char-syntax prev) '(?\) ?\" ?\|)))
      (delete-pair -1))
     ;; Delete following opening delimiter or quote.
     ((and next (memq (char-syntax next) '(?\( ?\" ?\|)))
      (delete-pair 1))
     ;; Inside a string: jump to start, handle prefixes, and delete.
     ((nth 3 ppss)
      (let ((start (nth 8 ppss)))
        (goto-char start)
        ;; Handle string prefixes (e.g., f"..." in Python).
        (unless (memq (char-after) '(?\" ?\|))
          (forward-char 1))
        (delete-pair 1)))
     ;; Fallback: jump out of current list and delete enclosing pair.
     (t
      (condition-case nil
          (progn
            (backward-up-list)
            (delete-pair 1))
        (scan-error (user-error "Not inside a deletable pair")))))))
#+end_src

(use-package paren
  :ensure nil
  :custom
  ;; Delay before highlighting matching delimiters.
  (show-paren-delay 0.05)
  ;; Highlight the opening delimiter when point is on the closing one.
  (show-paren-highlight-openparen t)
  ;; Highlight pairs when point is inside or in the periphery of the parens.
  (show-paren-when-point-inside-paren t)
  (show-paren-when-point-in-periphery t)
  ;; Highlight only the exact parenthesis characters, not the whole expression.
  (show-paren-style 'parenthesis)
  ;; Emacs 29+: Render off-screen context as a window overlay.
  ;; Bypasses the echo area to prevent clobbering ElDoc and Which-Key.
  (show-paren-context-when-offscreen 'overlay)
  :config
  (show-paren-mode 1)
  ;; Emacs 31+: Prevent phantom bracket highlighting inside strings and comments.
  (when (boundp 'show-paren-not-in-comments-or-strings)
    (setq show-paren-not-in-comments-or-strings t)))

(use-package rainbow-delimiters
  :defer t
  :custom-face
  ;; Tokyo Night depth mapping.
  (rainbow-delimiters-depth-1-face ((t (:foreground "#7aa2f7"))))
  (rainbow-delimiters-depth-2-face ((t (:foreground "#e0af68"))))
  (rainbow-delimiters-depth-3-face ((t (:foreground "#9ece6a"))))
  (rainbow-delimiters-depth-4-face ((t (:foreground "#1abc9c"))))
  (rainbow-delimiters-depth-5-face ((t (:foreground "#bb9af7"))))
  (rainbow-delimiters-depth-6-face ((t (:foreground "#9d7cd8"))))
  (rainbow-delimiters-depth-7-face ((t (:foreground "#ff9e64"))))
  (rainbow-delimiters-depth-8-face ((t (:foreground "#f7768e"))))
  (rainbow-delimiters-depth-9-face ((t (:foreground "#7aa2f7"))))
  :hook ((prog-mode . rainbow-delimiters-mode)
         (LaTeX-mode . rainbow-delimiters-mode))
  :config
  ;; Aborts activation in massive buffers; bypasses guard to allow explicit deactivation.
  (define-advice rainbow-delimiters-mode (:before-while (&optional arg) guard-large-files)
    (or (and arg (< (prefix-numeric-value arg) 1))
        (not (too-long-file-p)))))

** DONE Evil Textobj Tree-Sitter
Maps native Tree-sitter AST nodes to Evil operator-pending text objects and Unimpaired-style goto motions. Which-key annotations are sanitized to prevent UI clutter.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package evil-textobj-tree-sitter
  :defer t
  :after evil
  :config
  ;; Maps AST nodes directly to Evil's native operator-pending text-object keymaps.
  (define-key evil-inner-text-objects-map "f" (evil-textobj-tree-sitter-get-textobj "function.inner"))
  (define-key evil-outer-text-objects-map "f" (evil-textobj-tree-sitter-get-textobj "function.outer"))
  (define-key evil-inner-text-objects-map "F" (evil-textobj-tree-sitter-get-textobj "call.inner"))
  (define-key evil-outer-text-objects-map "F" (evil-textobj-tree-sitter-get-textobj "call.outer"))
  (define-key evil-inner-text-objects-map "C" (evil-textobj-tree-sitter-get-textobj "class.inner"))
  (define-key evil-outer-text-objects-map "C" (evil-textobj-tree-sitter-get-textobj "class.outer"))
  (define-key evil-outer-text-objects-map "c" (evil-textobj-tree-sitter-get-textobj "comment.outer"))
  (define-key evil-inner-text-objects-map "v" (evil-textobj-tree-sitter-get-textobj "conditional.inner"))
  (define-key evil-outer-text-objects-map "v" (evil-textobj-tree-sitter-get-textobj "conditional.outer"))
  (define-key evil-inner-text-objects-map "l" (evil-textobj-tree-sitter-get-textobj "loop.inner"))
  (define-key evil-outer-text-objects-map "l" (evil-textobj-tree-sitter-get-textobj "loop.outer"))
  (define-key evil-inner-text-objects-map "a" (evil-textobj-tree-sitter-get-textobj '("parameter.inner" "call.inner")))
  (define-key evil-outer-text-objects-map "a" (evil-textobj-tree-sitter-get-textobj '("parameter.outer" "call.outer")))

  ;; Dynamically registers named interactive commands for Unimpaired-style bracket navigation.
  (defun ar/ts-register-goto (key node &optional previous)
    (let* ((suffix (if previous "prev" "next"))
           (cmd-name (intern (format "ar/ts-goto-%s-%s"
                                     (string-replace "." "-" node)
                                     suffix)))
           (doc (format "Jump to %s %s node." (if previous "previous" "next") node)))
      (defalias cmd-name
        `(lambda ()
           (interactive)
           (evil-textobj-tree-sitter-goto-textobj ,node ,previous))
        doc)
      (general-define-key
       :states '(normal visual motion)
       key cmd-name)))

  (ar/ts-register-goto "]f" "function.outer")
  (ar/ts-register-goto "[f" "function.outer" t)
  (ar/ts-register-goto "]F" "call.outer")
  (ar/ts-register-goto "[F" "call.outer" t)
  (ar/ts-register-goto "]C" "class.outer")
  (ar/ts-register-goto "[C" "class.outer" t)
  (ar/ts-register-goto "]c" "comment.outer")
  (ar/ts-register-goto "[c" "comment.outer" t)
  (ar/ts-register-goto "]v" "conditional.outer")
  (ar/ts-register-goto "[v" "conditional.outer" t)
  (ar/ts-register-goto "]l" "loop.outer")
  (ar/ts-register-goto "[l" "loop.outer" t)
  (ar/ts-register-goto "]a" "parameter.outer")
  (ar/ts-register-goto "[a" "parameter.outer" t)

  ;; Strips verbose package prefixes and .inner/.outer suffixes from Which-Key popups.
  (with-eval-after-load 'which-key
    (add-to-list 'which-key-replacement-alist
                 '(("" . "\\`+?evil-textobj-tree-sitter-function--\\(.*\\)\\(?:\\.inner\\|\\.outer\\)") . (nil . "\\1")))))
#+end_src

Provides AST-aware structural manipulation via the Emacs 30 =treesit-thing= API. Refactoring operators are isolated within a Transient menu to prevent global keymap collisions.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package evil-ts-obj
  :ensure (evil-ts-obj :host github
                       :repo "dvzubarev/evil-ts-obj")
  :if emacs/>=30p
  :defer t
  :after evil
  :hook ((prog-mode . ar/evil-ts-obj-maybe-enable)
         (LaTeX-mode . evil-ts-obj-mode))
  :init
  ;; Guards activation against Lisp modes to prevent missing grammar warnings.
  (defun ar/evil-ts-obj-maybe-enable ()
    (unless (derived-mode-p 'lisp-mode 'emacs-lisp-mode 'lisp-interaction-mode 'lisp-data-mode)
      (evil-ts-obj-mode 1)))
  :config
  ;; Aborts activation in massive buffers; bypasses guard to allow explicit deactivation.
  (define-advice evil-ts-obj-mode (:before-while (&optional arg) guard-large-files)
    (or (and arg (< (prefix-numeric-value arg) 1))
        (not (too-long-file-p))))

  ;; Isolates structural mutations to prevent accidental execution during rapid typing.
  (with-eval-after-load 'transient
    (defun ar/ast-refactor-title ()
      (concat
       (if (fboundp 'nerd-icons-codicon)
           (nerd-icons-codicon "nf-cod-symbol_structure" :face 'transient-heading :v-adjust 0.02)
         "")
       (propertize " AST Refactor" 'face 'transient-heading)))

    (transient-define-prefix ar/ast-refactor-transient ()
      "AST Structural Refactoring"
      [:description ar/ast-refactor-title
       ["Drag & Swap"
        ("j" "Drag Down" evil-ts-obj-drag-down :transient t)
        ("k" "Drag Up" evil-ts-obj-drag-up :transient t)
        ("J" "Swap Down" evil-ts-obj-swap-dwim-down :transient t)
        ("K" "Swap Up" evil-ts-obj-swap-dwim-up :transient t)]
       ["Extract & Inject"
        ("l" "Extract Out" evil-ts-obj-extract-down-dwim :transient t)
        ("h" "Extract In" evil-ts-obj-extract-up-dwim :transient t)
        ("s" "Inject Down" evil-ts-obj-inject-down-dwim :transient t)
        ("S" "Inject Up" evil-ts-obj-inject-up-dwim :transient t)]
       ["Mutate"
        ("r" "Raise" evil-ts-obj-raise-dwim :transient t)
        ("c" "Clone After" evil-ts-obj-clone-after-dwim :transient t)
        ("C" "Clone Before" evil-ts-obj-clone-before-dwim :transient t)
        (">" "Slurp" evil-ts-obj-slurp :transient t)
        ("<" "Barf" evil-ts-obj-barf :transient t)
        ("<escape>" "" transient-quit-one :format " ")]])))
#+end_src

Exposes the Emacs 30 =treesit-thing= API via Unimpaired bracket motions. Provides O(1) C-level AST sibling traversal natively.

;; Emacs 30+: Built-in `-ts-mode` modes natively invoke `treesit-major-mode-setup`,
;; automatically overriding `forward-sexp-function` and `forward-sentence-function`.
;; Evil's `evil-forward-sexp` inherits this AST-awareness without manual rebinding.

  (general-define-key
   :states '(normal visual motion)
   "]s" (lambda () (interactive) (ignore-errors (treesit-thing-next 'sentence)))
   "[s" (lambda () (interactive) (ignore-errors (treesit-thing-prev 'sentence)))
   "]x" (lambda () (interactive) (ignore-errors (treesit-thing-next 'sexp)))
   "[x" (lambda () (interactive) (ignore-errors (treesit-thing-prev 'sexp)))))

** DONE Expreg
Provides O(1) AST-aware visual selection expansion via the native =treesit= engine. Integrates with =repeat-mode= for modifier-less tapping and preserves cursor origin on contraction.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package expreg
  :if (treesit-available-p)
  :defer t
  :commands (expreg-expand expreg-contract)
  :config
  ;; Restores exact cursor origin upon `C-g` (keyboard-quit) to prevent spatial drift.
  (setq expreg-restore-point-on-quit t)
  ;; Emacs 29+ `repeat-mode` integration for modifier-less expansion loops.
  (defvar-keymap expreg-repeat-map
    :doc "Keymap for repeating expreg commands."
    "+" #'expreg-expand
    "=" #'expreg-expand
    "-" #'expreg-contract
    "_" #'expreg-contract)
  (put 'expreg-expand 'repeat-map 'expreg-repeat-map)
  (put 'expreg-contract 'repeat-map 'expreg-repeat-map)
  ;; Visual State Routing: Hijacks `v` for seamless AST expansion loops.
  (general-define-key
   :states 'visual
   "v" #'expreg-expand)
  ;; Global Fallback: Maps `M-=` (Doom's expand-region mnemonic) and modifier combinations.
  (general-define-key
   :states '(normal visual motion)
   "M-=" #'expreg-expand
   "C-M-+" #'expreg-expand
   "C-M--" #'expreg-contract))
#+end_src

** DONE Custom Folding Functions
Unified folding dispatcher routing to Tree-sitter, Outline, Hideshow, or Vimish Fold based on buffer context.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defun ar/fold-toggle ()
  "Toggle code fold at point using the best available folding engine."
  (interactive)
  (cond ((bound-and-true-p treesit-fold-mode) (treesit-fold-toggle))
        ((bound-and-true-p outline-minor-mode) (outline-cycle))
        ((bound-and-true-p hs-minor-mode)
         ;; Emacs 31+: Prefer `hs-cycle` for intelligent visibility cycling.
         (if (fboundp 'hs-cycle) (hs-cycle) (hs-toggle-hiding)))
        ((fboundp 'vimish-fold-toggle)
         (condition-case nil (vimish-fold-toggle)
           (error (user-error "No foldable region at point"))))
        (t (user-error "No foldable region at point"))))

(defun ar/fold-open ()
  "Open code fold at point."
  (interactive)
  (cond ((bound-and-true-p treesit-fold-mode) (treesit-fold-open))
        ((bound-and-true-p outline-minor-mode) (outline-show-subtree))
        ((bound-and-true-p hs-minor-mode) (hs-show-block))
        ((fboundp 'vimish-fold-unfold) (vimish-fold-unfold))
        (t (user-error "No foldable region at point"))))

(defun ar/fold-close ()
  "Close code fold at point."
  (interactive)
  (cond ((bound-and-true-p treesit-fold-mode) (treesit-fold-close))
        ((bound-and-true-p outline-minor-mode) (outline-hide-subtree))
        ((bound-and-true-p hs-minor-mode) (hs-hide-block))
        (t (user-error "No foldable region at point"))))

(defun ar/fold-open-all ()
  "Open all code folds in the current buffer."
  (interactive)
  (cond ((bound-and-true-p treesit-fold-mode) (treesit-fold-open-all))
        ((bound-and-true-p outline-minor-mode) (outline-show-all))
        ((bound-and-true-p hs-minor-mode) (hs-show-all))
        ((fboundp 'vimish-fold-delete-all) (vimish-fold-delete-all)))
  (message "All folds opened"))

(defun ar/fold-close-all ()
  "Close all code folds in the current buffer."
  (interactive)
  (cond ((bound-and-true-p treesit-fold-mode) (treesit-fold-close-all))
        ((bound-and-true-p outline-minor-mode) (outline-hide-body))
        ((bound-and-true-p hs-minor-mode) (hs-hide-all)))
  (message "All folds closed"))
#+end_src

(use-package hideshow
  :ensure nil
  :defer t
  :commands (hs-toggle-hiding
             hs-hide-block
             hs-show-block
             hs-hide-all
             hs-show-all
             hs-hide-level
             hs-cycle
             hs-toggle-all)
  :custom

  (hs-display-lines-hidden t)

  :config

  (define-advice hs-minor-mode (:before-while (&optional arg) guard-large-files)
    (or (and arg (< (prefix-numeric-value arg) 1))
        (not (too-long-file-p)))))

** DONE Dtrt Indent
Heuristic indentation detection for programming buffers. Executes post-mode initialization to preempt LSP spawning and aborts on massive files.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package dtrt-indent
  :defer t
  :custom
  ;; Defers to SMIE indentation engines in supported modes.
  (dtrt-indent-run-after-smie t)
  ;; Synchronizes Emacs' global fallback indentation variable.
  (dtrt-indent-update-standard-indent t)
  ;; Restricts line scanning to prevent main-thread micro-stutters.
  (dtrt-indent-max-lines 2000)
  ;; Minimum confidence threshold for indentation detection.
  (dtrt-indent-min-quality 80.0)
  ;; Suppresses echo-area logging.
  (dtrt-indent-verbosity 0)
  :init
  (defun ar/dtrt-indent-maybe-enable ()
    "Enable `dtrt-indent-mode' in programming buffers, excluding structural exceptions."
    (when (and (derived-mode-p 'prog-mode 'conf-mode)
               (not (too-long-file-p))
               ;; Excludes Makefiles (requires hard tabs) and YAML (strict spacing).
               (not (apply #'derived-mode-p '(makefile-mode yaml-mode))))
      (dtrt-indent-mode 1)))
  ;; Executes after major-mode body but before mode hooks and LSP spawning.
  (add-hook 'change-major-mode-after-body-hook #'ar/dtrt-indent-maybe-enable)
  :config
  ;; Synchronizes Evil shift-width with the detected indentation offset.
  (add-hook 'dtrt-indent-hook
            (lambda () (setq-local evil-shift-width tab-width))))
#+end_src


** DONE Indent Bars
AST-aware vertical guides synchronizing depth colorization with rainbow-delimiters. Restricts activation to programming buffers to prevent Org-mode structural corruption.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package indent-bars
  :defer t
  :init
  ;; Blocks activation in massive files, child frames, fontification buffers, and Org buffers.
  (defun ar/indent-bars-should-enable-p ()
    "Return non-nil if indent-bars should be enabled in current buffer."
    (not (or (too-long-file-p)
             (frame-parameter nil 'parent-frame)
             (bound-and-true-p org-indent-mode)
             (string-prefix-p " *org-src-fontification:" (buffer-name))
             (derived-mode-p 'org-mode
                             'lisp-mode
                             'emacs-lisp-mode
                             'lisp-interaction-mode 'lisp-data-mode))))
  (defun ar/indent-bars-maybe-enable ()
    "Enable indent-bars-mode if conditions are met."
    (when (ar/indent-bars-should-enable-p)
      (indent-bars-mode 1)))
  :custom
  ;; Enforces AST structural boundaries and prevents string descent.
  (indent-bars-treesit-support t)
  (indent-bars-no-descend-lists t)
  (indent-bars-no-descend-string 'all)
  ;; Encapsulates multi-line AST nodes to prevent visual fragmentation.
  (indent-bars-treesit-wrap '((python arguments parameters argument_list parameter_list)
                              (c argument_list parameter_list)
                              (cpp argument_list parameter_list)
                              (rust arguments parameters)
                              (javascript arguments formal_parameters)
                              (typescript arguments formal_parameters)
                              (tsx arguments formal_parameters)))
  ;; Renders ghost bars on empty lines to connect structural blocks.
  (indent-bars-display-on-blank-lines t)
  ;; Unicode fallback for terminals and PGTK stipple failures.
  (indent-bars-no-stipple-char ?▍)
  ;; Tokyo Night depth palette matching rainbow-delimiters.
  (indent-bars-color-by-depth '(:palette ("#7aa2f7" "#e0af68" "#9ece6a" "#1abc9c" "#bb9af7" "#9d7cd8" "#ff9e64" "#f7768e") :blend 1))
  ;; High-contrast active scope tracking.
  (indent-bars-highlight-current-depth '(:color "#c0caf5" :blend 0.3 :width 0.25))
  :hook ((prog-mode conf-mode yaml-mode) . ar/indent-bars-maybe-enable)
  :config
  ;; Disables rendering inside magit-blame overlays.
  (with-eval-after-load 'magit-blame
    (add-to-list 'magit-blame-disable-modes 'indent-bars-mode)))
#+end_src

Provides O(1) line and region dragging. Automatically re-indents displaced lines to preserve structural integrity in whitespace-sensitive languages.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package move-text
  :defer t
  :commands (move-text-up move-text-down)
  :config
  ;; Re-indent the displaced line or region to preserve structural integrity.
  (defun ar/move-text-indent-region-advice (&rest ignored)
    "Re-indent the current line or active region after moving text."
    (let ((deactivate deactivate-mark))
      (if (region-active-p)
          (indent-region (region-beginning) (region-end))
        (indent-region (line-beginning-position) (line-end-position)))
      (setq deactivate-mark deactivate)))
  (advice-add #'move-text-up :after #'ar/move-text-indent-region-advice)
  (advice-add #'move-text-down :after #'ar/move-text-indent-region-advice))

(use-package hl-line
  :ensure nil
  :custom
  ;; Confines highlight strictly to the active window.
  (hl-line-sticky-flag nil)
  ;; Forces overlay behind syntax highlighting, diagnostics, and whitespace markers.
  (hl-line-overlay-priority -1)
  :config
  ;; Intercepts the render cycle to delete the overlay in excluded modes.
  (define-advice global-hl-line-highlight (:around (fn) guard-excluded-modes)
    (if (derived-mode-p 'dired-mode
                        'magit-mode
                        'compilation-mode
                        'special-mode
                        'dashboard-mode
                        'eshell-mode
                        'shell-mode
                        'term-mode
                        'vterm-mode
                        'eat-mode
                        'ghostel-mode
                        'ghostel-compile-view-mode
                        'pdf-view-mode
                        'treemacs-mode)
        (when global-hl-line-overlay (delete-overlay global-hl-line-overlay))
      (funcall fn)))
  ;; Destroys the overlay on Evil visual state entry.
  (add-hook 'evil-visual-state-entry-hook #'global-hl-line-unhighlight)
  ;; Redraws the overlay upon exiting Evil visual state.
  (add-hook 'evil-visual-state-exit-hook #'global-hl-line-highlight)
  ;; Activates synchronously to guarantee overlay registration.
  (global-hl-line-mode 1))

(use-package doom-modeline
  :init
  ;; Disables native size indicators and percentage formats before package load
  ;; to prevent doom-modeline from inheriting and rendering redundant segments.
  (size-indication-mode -1)
  (setq-default mode-line-percent-position nil)
  :custom
  ;; Visual dimensions and split-window overflow safeguard.
  (doom-modeline-height 25)
  (doom-modeline-bar-width 3)
  (doom-modeline-window-width-limit 90)
  ;; Project detection safely falls back to native `project.el'.
  (doom-modeline-project-detection 'auto)
  (doom-modeline-buffer-file-name-style 'relative-from-project)
  ;; Disables redundant size, encoding, and percentage indicators.
  (doom-modeline-buffer-file-size nil)
  (doom-modeline-buffer-encoding nil)
  (doom-modeline-percent-position nil)
  ;; Enables native Evil state, modal icons, and major-mode glyphs.
  (doom-modeline-modal t)
  (doom-modeline-modal-icon t)
  (doom-modeline-major-mode-icon t)
  ;; IDE synergy: LSP server status and active language version rendering.
  (doom-modeline-lsp t)
  (doom-modeline-env-version t)
  ;; Version control integration.
  (doom-modeline-vcs-icon t)
  (doom-modeline-vcs-max-length 0)
  ;; Prose word-count engine strictly confined to writing modes.
  (doom-modeline-enable-word-count t)
  (doom-modeline-continuous-word-count-modes '(markdown-mode gfm-mode org-mode))
  ;; Disables unused integrations to prevent background I/O overhead.
  (doom-modeline-github nil)
  (doom-modeline-mu4e nil)
  (doom-modeline-persp-name nil)
  (doom-modeline-minor-modes nil)
  :config
  ;; Activates the modeline engine directly; Elpaca guarantees deferred execution.
  (doom-modeline-mode 1))

** DONE Hide Modeline
Native redisplay concealment for terminal, agenda, and UI buffers. Replaces unmaintained dependencies with a zero-overhead buffer-local minor mode.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defvar-local ar/saved-mode-line-format nil "Cached modeline format.")
(defvar-local ar/saved-header-line-format nil "Cached header-line format.")
(defvar-local ar/saved-tab-line-format nil "Cached tab-line format.")

(define-minor-mode ar/hide-modeline-mode
  "Conceal the modeline, header-line, and tab-line in specific buffers."
  :init-value nil
  :lighter nil
  (if ar/hide-modeline-mode
      (progn
        ;; Cache original formats only if they are not already nil.
        (unless (eq mode-line-format nil)
          (setq-local ar/saved-mode-line-format mode-line-format))
        (unless (eq header-line-format nil)
          (setq-local ar/saved-header-line-format header-line-format))
        (unless (eq tab-line-format nil)
          (setq-local ar/saved-tab-line-format tab-line-format))
        ;; Force concealment.
        (setq-local mode-line-format nil
                    header-line-format nil
                    tab-line-format nil))
    ;; Restore cached formats upon deactivation.
    (setq-local mode-line-format ar/saved-mode-line-format
                header-line-format ar/saved-header-line-format
                tab-line-format ar/saved-tab-line-format))
  ;; Forces C-level redisplay to prevent phantom spacing artifacts.
  (force-mode-line-update))

(defun ar/enable-hide-modeline ()
  "Safely enable `ar/hide-modeline-mode' without toggling."
  (ar/hide-modeline-mode 1))

(dolist (mode '(eat-mode eshell-mode shell-mode term-mode vterm-mode
                embark-collect-mode lsp-ui-imenu-mode ghostel-mode
                ghostel-compile-view-mode pdf-annot-list-mode))
  (add-hook (intern (format "%s-hook" mode)) #'ar/enable-hide-modeline))
#+end_src

;; Hardcoded arguments bypass the interactive prefix parser to prevent sign inversion.

** DONE Avy
Provides an O(1) radix-tree jumping engine for long-distance spatial navigation. Configured with the =at-full= overlay style, home-row routing, proximity sorting, and on-demand multi-window expansion.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package avy
  :defer t
  :commands (avy-goto-char-2
             avy-goto-line
             avy-goto-word-1
             avy-goto-word-0
             avy-isearch
             avy-move-line
             avy-copy-line
             avy-kill-region
             avy-transpose-lines-in-region
             avy-move-region
             avy-copy-region
             avy-kill-ring-save-region)
  :bind (:map isearch-mode-map
         ("M-j" . avy-isearch))
  :custom
  ;; Path Generation: Standard shortest-path algorithm restricted to home-row.
  (avy-keys '(?a ?s ?d ?f ?g ?h ?j ?k ?l))
  ;; Visual Rendering: `at-full` preserves peripheral word context.
  (avy-style 'at-full)
  ;; Buffer Dimming: Forces focus strictly to the opaque neon spotlights.
  (avy-background t)
  ;; Scope Control: Defaults to active window; `C-u` expands search to all windows.
  (avy-all-windows nil)
  (avy-all-windows-alt t)
  ;; Candidate Validation: Renders overlay for single targets to prevent accidental jumps.
  (avy-single-candidate-jump nil)
  ;; Proximity Engine: Sorts candidates by physical distance to cursor.
  (avy-orders-alist '((avy-goto-char . avy-order-closest)
                      (avy-goto-word-0 . avy-order-closest)
                      (avy-goto-word-1 . avy-order-closest)))
  ;; Input Latency: Tightens the seek-then-jump response time.
  (avy-timeout-seconds 0.3)
  :custom-face
  ;; Opaque Spotlight Faces: 100% opaque backgrounds prevent ghosting artifacts.
  (avy-lead-face-0 ((t (:background "#f7768e" :foreground "#1a1b26" :weight bold))))
  (avy-lead-face-1 ((t (:background "#e0af68" :foreground "#1a1b26" :weight bold))))
  (avy-lead-face-2 ((t (:background "#9ece6a" :foreground "#1a1b26" :weight bold))))
  (avy-lead-face ((t (:background "#7dcfff" :foreground "#1a1b26" :weight bold)))))
#+end_src


** DONE Evil Easymotion
Wraps Avy into Evil's operator-pending grammar via a unified =gs= prefix. Bypasses =evilem-create= infinite loop traps by registering native Avy commands directly into Evil's C-level motion registry.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package evil-easymotion
  :after (evil avy)
  :config
  ;; Native Evil motion wrappers bypass `evilem-create` UI traps.
  (evil-define-motion ar/evil-avy-goto-char-2 ()
    "Jump to 2-char target."
    :type exclusive :jump t
    (avy-goto-char-2))
  (evil-define-motion ar/evil-avy-goto-line ()
    "Jump to visible line."
    :type line :jump t
    (avy-goto-line))
  (evil-define-motion ar/evil-avy-goto-char-timer ()
    "Jump to string target via timer."
    :type exclusive :jump t
    (avy-goto-char-timer))
  ;; Populate `evilem-map` under the "gs" prefix.
  (evilem-default-keybindings "gs")
  ;; Inject custom wrappers into the `gs` namespace.
  (define-key evilem-map "s" #'ar/evil-avy-goto-char-2)
  (define-key evilem-map "l" #'ar/evil-avy-goto-line)
  (define-key evilem-map "SPC" #'ar/evil-avy-goto-char-timer))
