** TODO Direnv
   #+begin_src emacs-lisp

(use-package envrc
  :hook (after-init . envrc-global-mode)
  :config
  ;; ==========================================
  ;; 1. HOOK TIMING (The LSP Race Condition Fix)
  ;; ==========================================
  ;; By default, `envrc` hooks into `after-change-major-mode-hook`. This runs
  ;; AFTER major mode hooks (like `python-mode-hook`). If `lsp-deferred` is
  ;; in `python-mode-hook`, the LSP server starts before `envrc` applies the
  ;; project's `VIRTUAL_ENV` or `PATH`.
  ;; It is moved to `change-major-mode-after-body-hook`, which runs BEFORE
  ;; major mode hooks, guaranteeing the environment is ready for LSP.
  (add-hook 'envrc-global-mode-hook
            (lambda ()
              ;; Emacs 30+ / newer envrc versions renamed this internal function.
              ;; The `fboundp` guard ensures compatibility across versions.
              (let ((fn (if (fboundp #'envrc-global-mode-enable-in-buffers)
                            #'envrc-global-mode-enable-in-buffers
                          #'envrc-global-mode-enable-in-buffer)))
                (if (not envrc-global-mode)
                    ;; Teardown safety: remove from BOTH hooks when disabled
                    ;; to prevent orphaned hook references.
                    (progn
                      (remove-hook 'change-major-mode-after-body-hook fn)
                      (remove-hook 'after-change-major-mode-hook fn))
                  (remove-hook 'after-change-major-mode-hook fn)
                  (add-hook 'change-major-mode-after-body-hook fn 100)))))

  ;; ==========================================
  ;; 2. ORG BABEL ENVIRONMENT PROPAGATION (Jupyter Support)
  ;; ==========================================
  ;; Upstream `envrc` advises `org-babel-eval`, but Jupyter kernel initialization
  ;; happens BEFORE that low-level shell spawn. Advising the higher-level
  ;; `org-babel-execute-src-block` ensures Jupyter kernels and persistent
  ;; sessions inherit the buffer-local `envrc` environment (e.g. VIRTUAL_ENV).
  (advice-add #'org-babel-execute-src-block :around #'envrc-propagate-environment)

  ;; ==========================================
  ;; 3. GRACEFUL FAILURE (Missing Executable Guard)
  ;; ==========================================
  ;; Prevents `envrc-global-mode` from spamming errors on every file open
  ;; if the `direnv` binary is not installed on the system (or over TRAMP).
  (define-advice envrc-global-mode (:before-while (&rest _) fail-gracefully)
    (or (executable-find envrc-direnv-executable)
        (ignore (message "Failed to locate direnv executable; aborting envrc-global-mode"))))

  ;; ==========================================
  ;; 4. DEBOUNCE INTERNAL BUFFERS
  ;; ==========================================
  ;; Prevents envrc from triggering inside its own *envrc* log buffers,
  ;; avoiding redundant processing or feedback loops.
  (define-advice envrc--update (:before-while (&rest _) debounce)
    (not (string-prefix-p "*envrc" (buffer-name))))

  ;; ==========================================
  ;; 5. LSP SERVER RESTART ON ENVRC RELOAD
  ;; ==========================================
  ;; When `.envrc` is updated, the environment changes, but the running LSP
  ;; server is still using the old environment. This advice catches the
  ;; update and restarts associated `lsp-mode` workspaces.
  (with-eval-after-load 'lsp-mode
    (when (fboundp 'envrc--mode-buffers)
      ;; Helper to restart LSP servers for a specific environment directory
      (defun ar/envrc--restart-lsp-workspaces (env-dir)
        "Restart `lsp-mode' workspaces associated with ENV-DIR."
        (let ((lsp-servers nil))
          (dolist (buf (envrc--mode-buffers))
            (with-current-buffer buf
              (let ((buf-env-dir (ignore-errors (envrc--find-env-dir))))
                (when (and buf-env-dir (string= buf-env-dir env-dir))
                  (when (bound-and-true-p lsp-mode)
                    (dolist (ws (lsp-workspaces))
                      (cl-pushnew ws lsp-servers :test #'equal)))))))
          (when lsp-servers
            (mapc #'lsp-workspace-restart lsp-servers)
            (message "Restarted %d LSP servers associated with direnv"
                     (length lsp-servers)))))

      ;; Standard reload vector (e.g., M-x envrc-reload)
      (define-advice envrc--update-env (:after (env-dir) restart-lsp)
        (ar/envrc--restart-lsp-workspaces env-dir))

      ;; Bulk reload vector (e.g., M-x envrc-reload-all)
      ;; Bypasses `envrc--update-env`, so it is caught here to restart
      ;; all unique LSP workspaces across all active envrc buffers.
      (define-advice envrc-reload-all (:after () restart-lsp-all)
        (let ((lsp-servers nil))
          (dolist (buf (envrc--mode-buffers))
            (with-current-buffer buf
              (when (bound-and-true-p lsp-mode)
                (dolist (ws (lsp-workspaces))
                  (cl-pushnew ws lsp-servers :test #'equal)))))
          (when lsp-servers
            (mapc #'lsp-workspace-restart lsp-servers)
            (message "Restarted %d LSP servers after direnv bulk reload"
                     (length lsp-servers))))))))
#+end_src

** DONE Universal Sidecar
Provides a per-frame sidebar buffer that dynamically renders contextual metadata sections for the focused window.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package universal-sidecar
  :defer t
  :commands (universal-sidecar-toggle
             universal-sidecar-insinuate
             universal-sidecar-uninsinuate
             universal-sidecar-refresh)
  :custom
  ;; Tie buffer name to the frame description.
  (universal-sidecar-buffer-name-format "*Sidecar: %F*")
  ;; Enable passive idle timer for content refresh.
  (universal-sidecar-enable-timer t)
  (universal-sidecar-refresh-time 5)
  :config
  ;; Route to a dedicated right-side window, isolated from Popper and `C-x o`.
  (add-to-list 'display-buffer-alist
               '("\\*Sidecar:.*\\*"
                 (display-buffer-in-side-window)
                 (side . right)
                 (window-width . 0.25)
                 (window-parameters (no-delete-other-windows . t)
                                    (no-other-window . t))))
  ;; Advise core navigation functions to trigger sidecar updates.
  (universal-sidecar-insinuate))
#+end_src

(use-package hl-todo
  :defer t
  :commands (hl-todo-rg-project hl-todo-rgrep hl-todo-next hl-todo-previous hl-todo-occur)

  :custom-face
  (hl-todo ((t (:inherit default :height 0.9 :width condensed :weight bold :underline nil :inverse-video nil))))

  :hook ((after-init . global-hl-todo-mode)
         (org-mode . hl-todo-mode))

  :custom
  (hl-todo-require-punctuation t)
  (hl-todo-highlight-punctuation "")

  :config
  (setq hl-todo-keyword-faces
        '(("TODO"       . "#e0af68")
          ("COMMENT"    . "#e0af68")
          ("FIXME"      . "#f7768e")
          ("BUG"        . "#f7768e")
          ("ISSUE"      . "#f7768e")
          ("PROG"       . "#7aa2f7")
          ("TRICK"      . "#ff9e64")
          ("WORKAROUND" . "#ff9e64")
          ("BENCHMARK"  . "#9ece6a")
          ("REVIEW"     . "#7dcfff")
          ("NOTE"       . "#bb9af7")
          ("DEPRECATED" . "#73daca")
          ("HACK"       . "#db4b4b")
          ("DEBUG"      . "#e0af68")
          ("STUB"       . "#e0af68")))

  (defun hl-todo-rg-project ()
    (interactive)
    (let ((regexp (concat "\\b(" (string-join (mapcar #'car hl-todo-keyword-faces) "|") ")\\b")))
      (cond
       ((fboundp 'consult-ripgrep)
        (consult-ripgrep nil regexp))
       ((fboundp 'hl-todo-rgrep)
        (hl-todo-rgrep))
       (t
        (user-error "Neither consult-ripgrep nor hl-todo-rgrep is available")))))

  ;; Safe registration of consult preview hooks
  (with-eval-after-load 'consult
    (when (boundp 'consult-preview-allowed-hooks)
      (add-to-list 'consult-preview-allowed-hooks 'hl-todo-mode)
      (add-to-list 'consult-preview-allowed-hooks 'global-hl-todo-mode))))
#+end_src

** TODO File Templates

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; File Templates - Automatic template insertion for new files
(defvar ar/file-templates-dir
  (expand-file-name "templates/" user-emacs-directory)
  "Directory containing yasnippet templates for file templates.")

(defvar ar/file-templates-default-trigger "__"
  "Default yasnippet trigger key for file template rules.")

;; Ensure templates directory is registered and loaded
(with-eval-after-load 'yasnippet
  (add-to-list 'yas-snippet-dirs ar/file-templates-dir t)
  (yas-reload-all))

(defvar ar/file-templates-alist
  '(;; --- Configuration Files ---
    (gitignore-mode :trigger "__gitignore")
    ("/docker-compose\\.y\\(a\\)?ml$" :mode yaml-mode :trigger "__docker_compose")
    ("/Makefile$" :mode makefile-gmake-mode :trigger "__Makefile")
    ("/\\.env$" :mode conf-mode :trigger "__env")

    ;; --- Dockerfile ---
    (dockerfile-mode :trigger "__Dockerfile")

    ;; --- Emacs Lisp ---
    ("/\\.dir-locals\\.el$" :trigger "__dir_locals")
    ("-test\\.el$" :mode emacs-ert-mode :trigger "__ert")
    (emacs-lisp-mode :trigger "__package")
    (snippet-mode :trigger "__snippet")

    ;; --- Python ---
    ("/setup\\.py$" :mode python-mode :trigger "__setup")
    ("/__init__\\.py$" :mode python-mode :trigger "__init")
    ("/test_.*\\.py$" :mode python-mode :trigger "__pytest")
    ;; Map all python modes to the 'python-mode' snippet table
    (python-base-mode :mode python-mode :trigger "__main")
    (python-mode :trigger "__main")
    (python-ts-mode :mode python-mode :trigger "__main")

    ;; --- Shell ---
    (fish-mode :trigger "__fish")
    (sh-mode :trigger "__sh_script")
    (bash-ts-mode :trigger "__sh_script")

    ;; --- Web / Data ---
    (yaml-mode :trigger "__yaml")
    (yaml-ts-mode :mode yaml-mode :trigger "__yaml")
    (json-mode :trigger "__json")
    (json-ts-mode :mode json-mode :trigger "__json")
    (toml-ts-mode :trigger "__toml")

    ;; --- Nix ---
    ("/flake\\.nix$" :mode nix-mode :trigger "__nix_flake")
    ("/shell\\.nix$" :mode nix-mode :trigger "__nix_shell")
    (nix-mode :trigger "__nix_default")

    ;; --- Org & Markdown ---
    ("/gtd/.*\\.org$" :mode org-mode :trigger "__org_gtd")
    (org-mode :trigger "__org_basic")
    ("/README\\.md$" :mode markdown-mode :trigger "__md_readme")
    (markdown-mode :trigger "__md_frontmatter")

    ;; --- Text / Misc ---
    ("/LICENSE$" :mode text-mode :trigger "__license")
    ("/COPYING$" :mode text-mode :trigger "__license")
    ("/robots\\.txt$" :mode text-mode :trigger "__robots")
    ("/humans\\.txt$" :mode text-mode :trigger "__humans")
    ("/CHANGELOG$" :mode text-mode :trigger "__changelog")
    (text-mode :trigger "__text_basic"))
  "Alist of file template rules.
Structure: (PREDICATE . PLIST)
PREDICATE: Regex string (filename) or Major Mode symbol.
PLIST keys:
  :trigger - Yasnippet trigger key.
  :mode    - The yasnippet table (subfolder name) to load/search.")

(defun ar/file-template-p (rule)
  "Check if RULE applies to the current buffer."
  (let ((pred (car rule)))
    (cond
     ((and (stringp pred) buffer-file-name)
      (string-match-p pred buffer-file-name))
     ((symbolp pred)
      (derived-mode-p pred))
     (t nil))))

(defun ar/file-templates-check ()
  "Check if current buffer should have a template inserted."
  (when (and (not (bound-and-true-p ar/file-templates-inhibit))
             buffer-file-name
             (not (file-exists-p buffer-file-name))
             (= (buffer-size) 0)
             (not buffer-read-only)
             (not (string-match-p "^[*[:space:]]" (buffer-name))))
    (ar/file-templates-apply)))

(defun ar/file-templates-apply ()
  "Find and expand a file template for the current buffer."
  (interactive)
  (when-let* ((rule (cl-find-if #'ar/file-template-p ar/file-templates-alist))
              (plist (cdr rule))
              (trigger (or (plist-get plist :trigger) ar/file-templates-default-trigger))
              (target-mode (or (plist-get plist :mode) major-mode)))

    ;; 1. Ensure Yasnippet is globally active
    (unless (bound-and-true-p yas-minor-mode)
      (yas-minor-mode 1))

    ;; 2. Activate the target mode to ensure its table is eligible for loading
    (yas-activate-extra-mode target-mode)

    ;; 3. Force load any pending JITs (fixes argument error)
    (when (fboundp 'yas--load-pending-jits)
      (yas--load-pending-jits))

    ;; 4. Try Lookup (with error suppression)
    (let ((template (yas-lookup-snippet trigger target-mode t)))
      ;; 5. Fallback: If not found, force a full reload and try one last time.
      ;; This handles cases where you just created a snippet file but didn't restart Emacs.
      (unless template
        (message "Template '%s' not found in memory. Reloading snippets..." trigger)
        (yas-reload-all)
        (setq template (yas-lookup-snippet trigger target-mode t)))

      ;; 6. Expand or Fail
      (if template
          (progn
            (message "Expanding template '%s' for %s..." trigger target-mode)
            (yas-expand-snippet (yas--template-content template) (point-min) (point-max)))
        (message "File Template Error: Snippet '%s' not found for mode '%s' in %s."
                 trigger target-mode ar/file-templates-dir)))))

;; Hook into file opening
(add-hook 'find-file-hook #'ar/file-templates-check)
#+end_src

** TODO Utility Functions
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defun ar/latex-indent-item-fn ()
  "Indent LaTeX itemize/enumerate/description environments."
  (save-match-data
    (let* ((re-beg "\\\\begin{")
           (re-end "\\\\end{")
           (re-env "\\(?:itemize\\|enumerate\\|description\\)")
           (indent (save-excursion
                     (when (looking-at (concat re-beg re-env "}"))
                       (end-of-line))
                     (LaTeX-find-matching-begin)
                     (+ LaTeX-item-indent (current-column))))
           (contin LaTeX-indent-level))
      (cond ((looking-at (concat re-beg re-env "}"))
             (or (save-excursion
                   (beginning-of-line)
                   (ignore-errors
                     (LaTeX-find-matching-begin)
                     (+ (current-column)
                        LaTeX-item-indent
                        LaTeX-indent-level
                        (if (looking-at (concat re-beg re-env "}"))
                            contin 0))))
                 indent))
            ((looking-at (concat re-end re-env "}"))
             (save-excursion
               (beginning-of-line)
               (ignore-errors
                 (LaTeX-find-matching-begin)
                 (current-column))))
            ((looking-at "\\\\item")
             (+ LaTeX-indent-level indent))
            (t (+ contin LaTeX-indent-level indent))))))

(defun ar/latex-fold-last-macro (&rest _)
  "Auto-fold the last LaTeX macro before point."
  (when (and (derived-mode-p 'LaTeX-mode)
             (bound-and-true-p TeX-fold-mode)
             (fboundp 'TeX-fold-region))
    (save-excursion
      (let ((start (search-backward "\\" (line-beginning-position) t)))
        (when start
          (TeX-fold-region start (1+ (point))))))))

(defun ar/latex-compile ()
  "Compile current LaTeX buffer."
  (interactive)
  (cond
   ((fboundp 'TeX-command-run-all)
    (TeX-command-run-all nil))
   ((fboundp 'TeX-command-master)
    (TeX-command-master))
   (t
    (user-error "No LaTeX compilation command available"))))

(defun ar/latex-find-master-file ()
  "Find and visit master file."
  (interactive)
  (if-let ((master (and (fboundp 'TeX-master-file)
                        (TeX-master-file))))
      (find-file master)
    (user-error "No master file found")))

(defun ar/latex-view-output ()
  "View compiled output."
  (interactive)
  (if (fboundp 'TeX-view)
      (TeX-view)
    (user-error "TeX-view function not available")))

(defun ar/latex-fold-toggle ()
  "Toggle folding of current element."
  (interactive)
  (if (not (bound-and-true-p TeX-fold-mode))
      (user-error "TeX-fold-mode is not active")
    (if (and (fboundp 'TeX-fold-item)
             (fboundp 'TeX-fold-clearout-item))
        (if (TeX-fold-item 'deep)
            (message "Folded")
          (TeX-fold-clearout-item)
          (message "Unfolded"))
      (user-error "TeX-fold functions not available"))))

(defun ar/latex-fold-all ()
  "Fold all elements in buffer."
  (interactive)
  (if (not (bound-and-true-p TeX-fold-mode))
      (user-error "TeX-fold-mode is not active. Enable it with M-x TeX-fold-mode")
    (if (fboundp 'TeX-fold-buffer)
        (progn
          (TeX-fold-buffer)
          (message "Folded all elements"))
      (user-error "TeX-fold-buffer function not available"))))

(defun ar/latex-fold-clear ()
  "Unfold all elements in buffer."
  (interactive)
  (if (not (bound-and-true-p TeX-fold-mode))
      (user-error "TeX-fold-mode is not active")
    (if (fboundp 'TeX-fold-clearout-buffer)
        (progn
          (TeX-fold-clearout-buffer)
          (message "Unfolded all elements"))
      (user-error "TeX-fold-clearout-buffer function not available"))))
#+end_src

** TODO Core AUCTeX
TODO: =add preview-auto.el from https://github.com/ultronozm/preview-auto.el=
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package tex
  :defer t
  :ensure auctex
  :hook ((LaTeX-mode . visual-line-mode)
         (LaTeX-mode . TeX-source-correlate-mode)
         (LaTeX-mode . outline-minor-mode)
         (LaTeX-mode . eglot-ensure))

  :init
  (setq jit-lock-chunk-size 4096)

  :config
  (setq TeX-engine 'luatex
        TeX-parse-self t
        TeX-auto-save t
        TeX-auto-local ".auctex-auto"
        TeX-style-local ".auctex-style"

        ;; Source Correlation
        TeX-source-correlate-mode t
        TeX-source-correlate-method 'synctex
        TeX-source-correlate-start-server nil

        ;; Workflow
        TeX-save-query nil
        TeX-process-asynchronous t
        TeX-display-help 'expert
        TeX-master t

        ;; Math Input
        TeX-electric-math nil
        TeX-electric-sub-and-superscript t

        ;; Folding

        TeX-fold-auto-reveal t

        ;; Font Lock
        font-latex-fontify-script 'invisible
        font-latex-fontify-sectioning 'color

        TeX-brace-indent-level 2)

  ;; TeX Command list
  (add-to-list 'TeX-command-list
               '("LuaLaTeXmk"
                 "latexmk -pdflua -pdflualatex='lualatex -shell-escape -interaction=nonstopmode' -f -outdir=%o %S"
                 TeX-run-TeX nil t
                 :help "Run latexmk with LuaLaTeX") t)

  (add-to-list 'TeX-command-list
               '("LaTeXmk-Watch"
                 "latexmk -pvc -pdflua -pdflualatex='lualatex -shell-escape -interaction=nonstopmode' -outdir=%o %S"
                 TeX-run-TeX nil t
                 :help "Run latexmk in continuous preview mode") t)

  (setq-default TeX-command-default "LuaLaTeXmk")

  ;; Clean Intermediate Files
  (setq TeX-clean-confirm nil)

  ;; Post-Compilation & Mode Associations
  (add-hook 'TeX-after-compilation-finished-functions #'TeX-revert-document-buffer)
  (add-to-list 'auto-mode-alist '("\\.map\\'" . TeX-mode))

  ;; TeX-MODE-HOOK (ispell & fill-nobreak setup)
  (add-hook 'TeX-mode-hook
            (lambda ()
              (setq-local ispell-parser 'tex)
              (visual-line-mode 1)
              (when (fboundp 'texmathp)
                (setq-local fill-nobreak-predicate
                            (cons #'texmathp fill-nobreak-predicate))))))
#+end_src

** TODO TeX Folding

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package tex-fold
  :ensure nil
  :after tex
  :hook (TeX-mode . ar/latex-fold-buffer-delayed)
  :hook (TeX-mode . TeX-fold-mode)
  :config
  (setq TeX-fold-auto nil
        TeX-fold-auto-reveal t)

  (defun ar/latex-fold-buffer-delayed ()
    "Fold buffer after idle delay."
    (run-with-idle-timer 0 nil 'TeX-fold-buffer))

  ;;(add-hook 'TeX-mode-hook #'ar/latex-fold-buffer-delayed)

  ;; Fold after AUCTeX macro insertions
  (add-hook 'TeX-fold-mode-hook
            (lambda ()
              (advice-add 'TeX-insert-macro :after #'ar/latex-fold-last-macro)))

  ;; Fold after CDLaTeX macros
  (with-eval-after-load 'cdlatex
    (add-hook 'TeX-fold-mode-hook
              (lambda ()
                (advice-add 'cdlatex-math-symbol :after #'ar/latex-fold-last-macro)
                (advice-add 'cdlatex-math-modify :after #'ar/latex-fold-last-macro))))

  ;; Fold after snippet expansion
  (with-eval-after-load 'yasnippet
    (defun ar/latex-fold-snippet-contents ()
      "Fold contents of last expanded snippet."
      (when (and (derived-mode-p 'LaTeX-mode)
                 (bound-and-true-p TeX-fold-mode)
                 yas-snippet-beg
                 yas-snippet-end)
        (TeX-fold-region yas-snippet-beg yas-snippet-end)))

    (add-hook 'yas-after-exit-snippet-hook #'ar/latex-fold-snippet-contents)))
#+end_src

** TODO Markdown
Configures a high-perf Markdown IDE via =gfm-mode= for native GFM syntax (intra-word underscores, strikethroughs, task lists). Toolchain:
- =marksman= (=lsp-mode=): Structural diagnostics (links/headings) + completions.
- =markdownlint-cli2= (=flycheck=): Stylistic linting (line length, heading increments).
- =prettierd= (=apheleia=): Async format-on-save.

*** Core Configuration & Appear Mode
Maps Markdown to =gfm-mode= with native rendering. Defines and hooks the O(1) Appear engine into =gfm-mode=.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package markdown-mode
  :defer t
  ;; FORCE GFM: Map all standard markdown extensions exclusively to gfm-mode.
  :mode (("README\\.md\\'" . gfm-mode)
         ("\\.md\\'" . gfm-mode)
         ("\\.markdown\\'" . gfm-mode))
  :init
  ;; Core Rendering & Behavior
  (setq markdown-italic-underscore t
        markdown-asymmetric-header t
        markdown-fontify-code-blocks-natively t
        markdown-gfm-uppercase-checkbox t
        markdown-enable-math nil
        markdown-hide-urls t
        markdown-hide-markup t)

  ;; List & Indentation
  (setq markdown-list-indent-width 2)

  ;; Performance: Disable expensive whole-line and block fontifications
  (setq markdown-fontify-whole-heading-line nil
        markdown-fontify-quote-and-verse-blocks nil)

  :hook ((gfm-mode . lsp-deferred)
         (gfm-mode . visual-line-mode)
         (gfm-mode . ar/markdown-appear-mode))

  :config
  ;; ==========================================
  ;; LSP INTEGRATION (Marksman via lsp-marksman)
  ;; ==========================================
  (require 'lsp-marksman)
  (with-eval-after-load 'lsp-mode
    (add-to-list 'lsp-language-id-configuration '(gfm-mode . "markdown"))
    (add-to-list 'lsp-language-id-configuration '(markdown-mode . "markdown")))

  ;; ==========================================
  ;; APHELEIA (Formatting via Prettierd)
  ;; ==========================================
  (with-eval-after-load 'apheleia
    (setf (alist-get 'prettierd apheleia-formatters)
          '("prettierd" filepath))
    (setf (alist-get 'gfm-mode apheleia-mode-alist) '(prettierd))
    (setf (alist-get 'markdown-mode apheleia-mode-alist) '(prettierd)))

  ;; TODO Renable when setting up flycheck
  ;; ;; ==========================================
  ;; ;; FLYCHECK (Linting via Markdownlint-CLI2 + LSP)
  ;; ;; ==========================================
  ;; (with-eval-after-load 'flycheck
  ;;   (flycheck-define-checker markdown-markdownlint-cli2
  ;;     "A Markdown syntax and style checker using markdownlint-cli2."
  ;;     :command ("markdownlint-cli2" source-original)
  ;;     :error-patterns
  ;;     ((warning line-start
  ;;               (file-name) ":" line ":" column " "
  ;;               (id (one-or-more (not (any " ")))) " "
  ;;               (message (one-or-more (not (any "\n"))))
  ;;               line-end))
  ;;     :modes (markdown-mode gfm-mode))

  ;;   (add-to-list 'flycheck-checkers 'markdown-markdownlint-cli2)

  ;;   ;; THE DEFINITIVE FIX FOR "lsp is not a valid syntax checker":
  ;;   ;; lsp-mode registers the `lsp` checker dynamically via `lsp-diagnostics-flycheck-enable`
  ;;   ;; ONLY when an LSP server connects to a buffer. If we try to chain `lsp` globally
  ;;   ;; before that happens, Flycheck throws an error because `lsp` isn't in its registry yet.
  ;;   ;; By explicitly requiring `lsp-diagnostics` and calling the enable function here,
  ;;   ;; we force the `lsp` checker to register immediately, making it safe to chain.
  ;;   ;; (This exact same logic fixes Python/Ruff/Mypy chaining issues).
  ;;   (with-eval-after-load 'lsp-mode
  ;;     (require 'lsp-diagnostics)
  ;;     (lsp-diagnostics-flycheck-enable)
  ;;     (flycheck-add-next-checker 'lsp 'markdown-markdownlint-cli2)))

  ;; ==========================================
  ;; O(1) MARKDOWN APPEAR ENGINE
  ;; ==========================================
  (defvar-local ar/md-appear--last-bounds nil
    "Cached (start . end) of the currently revealed markdown element.")

  (defun ar/md-appear--post-command ()
    "O(1) Markdown Appear engine. Bypasses regex parsing entirely."
    ;; 1. THE SPATIAL GUARD (O(1) Integer Math)
    (if (and ar/md-appear--last-bounds
             (>= (point) (car ar/md-appear--last-bounds))
             (<= (point) (cdr ar/md-appear--last-bounds)))
        nil ; Do nothing, stay inside the revealed zone.

      ;; 2. CURSOR MOVED: Handle leaving the old element.
      (when ar/md-appear--last-bounds
        (font-lock-flush (car ar/md-appear--last-bounds)
                         (cdr ar/md-appear--last-bounds))
        (setq ar/md-appear--last-bounds nil))

      ;; 3. PROPERTY INTERROGATION (O(1) C-Level Lookup)
      (let ((inv (get-text-property (point) 'invisible))
            (disp (get-text-property (point) 'display))
            (comp (get-text-property (point) 'composition)))

        (when (or inv disp comp)
          ;; 4. C-LEVEL BOUNDARY SCANNING
          (let* ((prop (cond (inv 'invisible) (disp 'display) (t 'composition)))
                 (start (previous-single-char-property-change (point) prop))
                 (end   (next-single-char-property-change (point) prop)))

            (remove-text-properties start end '(invisible nil display nil composition nil))
            (setq ar/md-appear--last-bounds (cons start end)))))))

  (define-minor-mode ar/markdown-appear-mode
    "O(1) dynamic markup revealing for Markdown/GFM modes."
    :init-value nil
    :lighter " MD-Appear"
    (if ar/markdown-appear-mode
        (add-hook 'post-command-hook #'ar/md-appear--post-command nil t)
      (remove-hook 'post-command-hook #'ar/md-appear--post-command t)
      (when ar/md-appear--last-bounds
        (font-lock-flush (car ar/md-appear--last-bounds)
                         (cdr ar/md-appear--last-bounds))
        (setq ar/md-appear--last-bounds nil))
      (font-lock-flush (point-min) (point-max))))

  ;; ==========================================
  ;; IMENU & NAVIGATION
  ;; ==========================================
  (setq markdown-imenu-generic-expression
        '(("^\\(.*\\)[ \t]*\n=+$" 1)
          ("^\\(.*\\)[ \t]*\n-+$" 1)
          ("^\\(#+ .*\\)" 1))))
#+end_src

*** Markdown TOC
Auto-generates and syncs heading-derived TOC.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package markdown-toc
  :defer t
  :after markdown-mode
  :hook (gfm-mode . markdown-toc-mode)
  :config
  (setq markdown-toc-header-toc-title "\n## Table of Contents"
        markdown-toc-user-toc-structure-manipulation-fn 'cdr))
#+end_src


* TODO XML Document Viewer
Native C-level XML ingestion and headless AST transformation. Renders XML state and protocol files as paginated, word-processor-style Org buffers utilizing proportional typography, centered margins, and native =dom.el= primitives.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(require 'dom)

;; ==========================================
;; RECURSIVE AST TRANSFORMATION ENGINE
;; ==========================================
(defun ar/xml-to-org (node &optional depth)
  "Recursively convert XML DOM NODE to Org-mode syntax.
DEPTH dictates the heading asterisk count."
  (let ((depth (or depth 1)))
    (cond
     ((stringp node) (string-trim node))
     ((consp node)
      (let* ((tag (dom-tag node))
             (attrs (dom-attributes node))
             (children (dom-children node))
             (title (or (alist-get 'title attrs)
                        (alist-get 'name attrs)
                        (alist-get 'id attrs)))
             (child-texts (seq-filter
                           (lambda (s) (not (string-empty-p (string-trim s))))
                           (mapcar (lambda (c) (ar/xml-to-org c (1+ depth))) children))))
        (pcase tag
          ;; Root and structural containers
          ((or 'project_state 'system_prompt_protocol 'metadata)
           (mapconcat #'identity child-texts "\n"))
          ;; Primary headings
          ((or 'section 'group 'critical_instructions 'core_architectural_rules
               'editor_behaviour_paradigm 'negative_constraints 'edge_cases_and_deferred_issues
               'architectural_decisions 'pending_decisions_and_todos 'overall_progress
               'immediate_structural_action_plan 'remaining_work_checklists 'global_questions
               'strict_greenlight_protocol 'formatting_rules 'architectural_constraints
               'strict_negative_constraints 'research_mandates 'state_generation
               'initialization_protocol)
           (let ((heading (concat (make-string depth ?*) " "
                                  (or title (replace-regexp-in-string "_" " " (symbol-name tag))))))
             (concat heading "\n\n" (mapconcat #'identity child-texts "\n") "\n")))
          ;; List items
          ((or 'rule 'constraint 'step 'task 'sub_rule 'mandate 'action 'todo 'issue 'decision 'question)
           (let ((item-body (mapconcat #'identity child-texts " ")))
             (concat "- " (when title (concat "*" title "*: ")) (string-trim item-body) "\n")))
          ;; Sub-headings (Sessions)
          ('session
           (let ((session-id (alist-get 'id attrs)))
             (concat (make-string (1+ depth) ?*) " Session " session-id "\n"
                     (mapconcat #'identity child-texts "\n") "\n")))
          ;; Text blocks and paragraphs
          ((or 'description 'preamble 'summary 'state 'trigger 'exact_phrase 'condition
               'wrapper 'internal_syntax 'tone_and_voice 'documentation_scope 'completeness
               'header_status 'greenlight_reminder)
           (concat (string-trim (mapconcat #'identity child-texts " ")) "\n"))
          ;; Document metadata
          ((or 'title 'version 'date)
           (let ((body (string-trim (mapconcat #'identity child-texts " "))))
             (pcase tag
               ('title (concat "#+TITLE: " body "\n"))
               ('version (concat "#+SUBTITLE: Version " body "\n"))
               ('date (concat "#+DATE: " body "\n")))))
          ;; Fallback passthrough
          (_ (mapconcat #'identity child-texts "\n")))))
     (t ""))))

;; ==========================================
;; WORD PROCESSOR VISUAL PARADIGM & ACTIONS
;; ==========================================
(defvar-local ar/xml-source-buffer nil
  "Source XML buffer for live refresh.")

(defun ar/xml-refresh-document ()
  "Refresh the current Org document from the source XML buffer."
  (interactive)
  (unless (buffer-live-p ar/xml-source-buffer)
    (user-error "Source XML buffer no longer exists"))
  (let ((inhibit-read-only t)
        (xml-dom (with-current-buffer ar/xml-source-buffer
                   (save-excursion
                     (goto-char (point-min))
                     (libxml-parse-xml-region (point-min) (point-max)))))
        (org-text (concat "#+LATEX_CLASS: cas-dc\n#+LATEX_COMPILER: lualatex\n"
                          (ar/xml-to-org xml-dom))))
    (erase-buffer)
    (insert org-text)
    (goto-char (point-min))
    (org-overview)
    (message "Document refreshed.")))

(defun ar/export-document-to-pdf ()
  "Export the current hidden Org buffer to PDF and open via `pdf-tools'."
  (interactive)
  (unless (fboundp 'org-latex-export-to-pdf)
    (user-error "Org LaTeX export engine unavailable"))
  (let* ((temp-org (make-temp-file "xml-doc-" ".org"))
         (temp-pdf (concat (file-name-sans-extension temp-org) ".pdf")))
    (write-region (point-min) (point-max) temp-org nil 'silent)
    (with-current-buffer (find-file-noselect temp-org)
      (unwind-protect
          (org-latex-export-to-pdf)
        (kill-buffer)))
    (when (file-exists-p temp-pdf)
      (find-file temp-pdf))))

(defvar ar/xml-document-viewer-map
  (let ((map (make-sparse-keymap)))
    (set-keymap-parent map org-mode-map)
    (define-key map (kbd "q") #'quit-window)
    (define-key map (kbd "e") #'ar/export-document-to-pdf)
    (define-key map (kbd "r") #'ar/xml-refresh-document)
    map)
  "Keymap for the rendered XML document viewer.")

;;;###autoload
(defun ar/view-xml-as-document ()
  "Parse current XML buffer into a paginated Org-mode word processor view."
  (interactive)
  (unless (and (fboundp 'libxml-parse-xml-region) (libxml-available-p))
    (user-error "Emacs lacks libxml support; cannot parse XML"))
  (let* ((source-buf (current-buffer))
         (xml-dom (save-excursion
                    (goto-char (point-min))
                    (libxml-parse-xml-region (point-min) (point-max))))
         (org-text (concat "#+LATEX_CLASS: cas-dc\n#+LATEX_COMPILER: lualatex\n"
                           (ar/xml-to-org xml-dom)))
         (buf-name (format "*Document: %s*" (file-name-base (or (buffer-file-name) (buffer-name)))))
         (buf (get-buffer-create buf-name)))
    (with-current-buffer buf
      (let ((inhibit-read-only t))
        (erase-buffer)
        (insert org-text)
        (goto-char (point-min))
        (org-mode)
        (use-local-map ar/xml-document-viewer-map)
        (setq-local ar/xml-source-buffer source-buf)
        (setq-local org-imenu-depth 8)
        (visual-line-mode 1)
        (variable-pitch-mode 1)
        (goto-address-mode 1)
        (setq-local show-trailing-whitespace nil)
        ;; Centered margins via Olivetti
        (when (fboundp 'olivetti-mode)
          (olivetti-mode 1)
          (setq-local olivetti-body-width 90))
        ;; Modern typography via Org-Modern
        (when (fboundp 'org-modern-mode)
          (org-modern-mode 1))
        ;; Inline LaTeX rendering
        (when (fboundp 'org-fragtog-mode)
          (org-fragtog-mode 1))
        ;; Predictable folding state
        (org-overview)
        (read-only-mode 1)
        (setq-local cursor-type nil)))
    (switch-to-buffer buf)
    (message "Document rendered. [q]uit | [e]xport PDF | [r]efresh")))
#+end_src

          ;; ADDED: Transient buffer
          "^\\*transient.*"))
  :config
  (with-no-warnings
    (defun my-popper-fit-window-height (win)
      "Adjust the height of popup window WIN to fit the buffer's content."
      (let ((desired-height (floor (/ (frame-height) 3))))
        (fit-window-to-buffer win desired-height desired-height)))
    (setq popper-window-height #'my-popper-fit-window-height)
    (defun popper-close-window-hack (&rest _args)
      "Close popper window via `C-g'."
      (when (and (not (region-active-p))
                 popper-open-popup-alist)
        (let ((window (caar popper-open-popup-alist))
              (buffer (cdar popper-open-popup-alist)))
          (when (and (window-live-p window)
                     (buffer-live-p buffer)
                     (not (with-current-buffer buffer
                            (derived-mode-p 'eshell-mode
                                            'shell-mode
                                            'term-mode
                                            'vterm-mode
                                            'eat-mode))))
            (delete-window window)))))
    (advice-add #'keyboard-quit :before #'popper-close-window-hack)))
#+end_src


** DONE Windmove
Native directional window navigation. Enables wrap-around physics for spatial traversal across frame edges.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; Enable wrap-around for directional window navigation.
;; Evil's `evil-window-left/right/up/down` commands natively delegate
;; to `windmove`, automatically inheriting this wrap-around behavior
;; for both `C-w h/j/k/l` and `SPC w h/j/k/l` leader bindings.
(setq windmove-wrap-around t)
#+end_src

** DONE Window Operations
Centralized leader bindings for spatial window traversal, splitting, and ace-window routing.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(ar/global-leader
  "w"           '(:ignore t :wk "window")
  ;; Ace Window Routing
  "w w"         '(ace-window :wk "Ace window")
  "w d"         '(ace-delete-window :wk "Ace delete window")
  "w m"         '(ace-delete-other-windows :wk "Maximize")
  "w x"         '(ace-swap-window :wk "Ace swap window")
  ;; Native Splitting
  "w s"         '(split-window-below :wk "Split below")
  "w v"         '(split-window-right :wk "Split right")
  ;; Directional Navigation
  "w <left>"    '(evil-window-left :wk "Window left")
  "w <down>"    '(evil-window-down :wk "Window down")
  "w <up>"      '(evil-window-up :wk "Window up")
  "w <right>"   '(evil-window-right :wk "Window right")
  ;; Directional Transposition
  "w S-<left>"  '(evil-window-move-far-left :wk "Move far left")
  "w S-<down>"  '(evil-window-move-very-bottom :wk "Move bottom")
  "w S-<up>"    '(evil-window-move-very-top :wk "Move top")
  "w S-<right>" '(evil-window-move-far-right :wk "Move far right")
  ;; Utilities & Winner Mode
  "w ="         '(balance-windows :wk "Balance windows")
  "w u"         '(winner-undo :wk "Winner undo")
  "w r"         '(winner-redo :wk "Winner redo")
  "w t"         '(toggle-window-split :wk "Toggle split")
  "w _"         '(split-window-vertically-instead :wk "Split vertically instead")
  "w |"         '(split-window-horizontally-instead :wk "Split horizontally instead"))
#+end_src

 "]]" #'eshell-next-prompt)
